### [1] [Proactive Road Safety Intervention in Australia: Predicting Risky Driving Hotspots from Connected Vehicle Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16913)
*Adriana-Simona Mihăiţă,Clarence Cheung,Artur Grigorev,Tuo Mao,David Lillo-Trynes*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 利用悉尼联网车辆遥测数据，通过g力阈值量化危险驾驶事件，构建时空热图，并对比八种预测模型，发现ARIMA以最低MAE（162.21）与LSTM相当，优于集成方法，识别出CBD、Parramatta等持续高风险区域，支持主动式道路安全干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统道路安全监测依赖事后事故记录，反应滞后，缺乏在伤亡发生前主动识别高风险位置和危险驾驶行为的方法，本研究旨在利用联网车辆数据填补这一关键空白。

Method: 基于大悉尼地区联网车辆遥测数据，以g力阈值（急刹车>0.6g、急转弯>0.47g、急加速>0.5g）量化危险驾驶，构建局地政府区域（LGA）级别的时空热图，并基准测试八种模型，涵盖集成学习（RF、XGBoost、LightGBM）、深度学习（LSTM、N-BEATS）和经典时间序列（ARIMA、ETS、Prophet）。

Result: ARIMA取得最低平均绝对误差（MAE=162.21），与LSTM（163.92）相当，优于所有集成方法，N-BEATS为180.75；悉尼内城区和西区LGA（CBD、Parramatta、Bankstown）被识别为持续高风险区域。

Conclusion: 在训练数据量有限时，简约时间序列模型与深度学习方法竞争力相当，验证了基于物联网的联网车辆数据在支持主动道路安全干预方面的潜力，并建议对高风险区域实施针对性政策。

Abstract: Road safety monitoring has historically been reactive, relying on crash-record analysis after fatalities and injuries have already occurred. Proactive identification of high-risk locations and dangerous driving behaviour before incidents occur is a critical but underexplored challenge. This paper addresses this gap using connected vehicle telemetry data from Greater Sydney, Australia, to detect and forecast near-miss risky driving events at the Local Government Area (LGA) level. Risky driving is quantified through g-force thresholds (hard braking >0.6g, harsh cornering >0.47g, harsh acceleration >0.5g), and spatio-temporal heatmaps are constructed to identify high-risk zones. Eight predictive models are benchmarked across three families: ensemble learning (Random Forests, XGBoost, LightGBM), deep learning (LSTM, N-BEATS), and classical time-series methods (ARIMA, Exponential Smoothing, Prophet). ARIMA achieves the lowest mean absolute error (MAE: 162.21), performing comparably to LSTM (MAE: 163.92) and outperforming all ensemble methods, with N-BEATS reaching an MAE of 180.75. These results demonstrate that parsimonious time-series models are competitive with deep learning approaches when training data volume is limited. The study highlights the potential of IoT-based connected vehicle data to support proactive road safety interventions, with Sydney's inner and western LGAs (CBD, Parramatta, Bankstown) identified as persistent high-risk zones warranting targeted policy action.

</details>


### [2] [Quantum-Safe Web Service Architecture Using Time-Based One-Time Passwords](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16961)
*Abel C. H. Chen*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于后量子密码（PQC）与HMAC驱动TOTP的量子安全Web服务架构，并对比SHA-2、SHA-3、Ascon-Hash256和SM3在不同硬件条件下的计算性能，为未来部署提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OTP虽常用于多因素认证，但在自动化系统互操作场景中需TOTP保障安全，而现有TOTP依赖的哈希算法与密码体系面临量子计算威胁，亟需量子安全的Web服务架构。

Method: 采用PQC实现TLS/HTTPS传输安全，结合HMAC驱动的TOTP实现Web服务安全；实验对比SHA-2、SHA-3、Ascon-Hash256、SM3在多种硬件资源下的计算耗时。

Result: 实验评估了四种哈希算法在不同硬件条件下的计算性能，量化了各算法所需时间差异，具体数值需见原文。

Conclusion: 该量子安全架构可行，且算法性能随硬件条件变化，未来部署需根据资源需求选择合适哈希算法。研究为后量子时代的Web服务安全提供了实践参考。

Abstract: One-Time Passwords (OTPs) have become a common option for multi-factor authentication in several applications. For instance, during website login processes, OTPs are often used in conjunction with traditional text-based usernames and passwords to verify whether the access request originates from a legitimate human user rather than an automated agent. However, in scenarios involving automated connections and system-to-system interoperability, Time-Based One-Time Passwords (TOTPs) may be required to establish secure connections and access Web Services (WSs). Therefore, this study focuses on exploring the development of a quantum-safe web service architecture. The proposed approach achieves transmission security management by implementing Transport Layer Security (TLS) and HyperText Transfer Protocol Secure (HTTPS) based on Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC). Furthermore, web service security management is realized through the construction of keyed-Hash Message Authentication Code (HMAC)-driven TOTPs. Within the experimental environment, this study evaluates and compares the computational performance of the Secure Hash Algorithm-2 (SHA-2), SHA-3, Ascon-Hash256, and SM3. The required computation time under different hardware resource conditions is analyzed for future web service deployment.

</details>


### [3] [Benchmarking Classical and Transformer-Based Models for Document Sensitivity Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16928)
*Aleesha Zainab,Muhammad Ahmed Khalid,Faheem Ullah Khan,Asifullah Khan*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文针对组织文档自动敏感性分类中的标签泄漏问题，构建了泄漏控制的Strategic 16K语料库（源自WikiLeaks PlusD的16,000份外交电文），提出系统性泄漏清除协议，并基准测试了六种模型。BERT表现最佳（准确率89.14%，F1=89.33%），TF-IDF+逻辑回归以更低成本取得强基线。这是首个明确控制泄漏条件的可复现基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档敏感性分类至关重要，但AI方法依赖训练数据完整性。标签泄漏（文档中残留的分类标记）使模型利用表面捷径而非学习真实内容信号，导致性能估计虚高且不可靠。现有研究对此问题报道不足，缺乏泄漏控制的基准。

Method: 构建Strategic 16K语料库（16,000篇外交电文），设计三步泄漏清除协议（识别并移除三类残留分类标记），并系统评估六种模型架构：经典机器学习（如TF-IDF+逻辑回归）和Transformer（如BERT、ELECTRA）。

Result: 清洁基准下，BERT最优（Accuracy=89.14%，F1=89.33%），ELECTRA次之（Accuracy=88.57%，F1=88.90%）。经典模型中TF-IDF+逻辑回归以显著更低计算成本获最强性能。

Conclusion: 建立了首个在明确泄漏控制条件下从WikiLeaks PlusD构建的完全可复现的敏感性分类基准，证明泄漏控制对可靠性能评估的必要性，并提供了低成本强基线模型。

Abstract: Automatic sensitivity classification of organizational documents is a critical yet underserved problem, where the consequences of misclassification range from regulatory violations to security breaches. While AI-based approaches offer a scalable alternative to manual review, their reliability depends fundamentally on the integrity of training data. A pervasive but underreported problem in this domain is label leakage: residual classification markers embedded within document bodies that allow models to exploit surface shortcuts rather than learning genuine content-based sensitivity signals, producing performance estimates that are inflated and unreliable. This paper addresses this problem by introducing Strategic 16K, a carefully constructed, leakage-controlled corpus of 16,000 diplomatic cables sourced from the WikiLeaks Public Library of US Diplomacy (PlusD), and presents a systematic benchmark evaluating six model architectures spanning classical machine learning and transformer-based approaches. We document an extended leakage removal protocol that identifies and eliminates three categories of residual classification markers embedded within document bodies. On the clean benchmark, BERT achieves the strongest performance (Accuracy = 89.14%, F1 = 89.33%), followed by ELECTRA (Accuracy = 88.57%, F1 = 88.90%). Among classical models, TF-IDF with Logistic Regression achieves the strongest performance at significantly lower computational cost. These results constitute the first fully reproducible sensitivity classification benchmark constructed under explicit leakage-controlled conditions from WikiLeaks PlusD.

</details>


### [4] [Probing the Prefill: Detecting Code Vulnerabilities via Latent Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16970)
*Alizishaan Khatri*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文探究LLM在阅读C/C++代码时，其隐藏激活是否携带漏洞信号。通过从四种LLM提取最后一层prefill token激活并训练MLP探针，在四个基准上平均F1达41.7%，其中Devign上最佳探针（68.8% F1）媲美微调分类器SOTA，但更难基准上仍落后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码漏洞防御多为事后筛查（如静态分析、微调分类器或LLM裁判），忽略了生成模型自身的内部状态。本文旨在验证一个更直接的问题：LLM读取代码时，其隐藏激活是否已包含漏洞相关信息，为轻量级、模型原生漏洞检测提供依据。

Method: 从Granite-4.1-8B、Qwen3.5-9B、Qwen3.6-27B、Gemma-4-12B四个LLM（覆盖三个家族）中提取最后prefill token的激活，训练MLP探针（参数13.4–16.0M，不足基座模型0.2%）。在Devign、Big-Vul、Draper VDISC、PrimeVul四个函数级C/C++基准上评估。

Result: 探针平均F1为41.7%。在Devign上最佳探针（Qwen3.5-9B）达68.8% F1，超过微调分类器SOTA（67.9%）；但在更难、更不平衡的Big-Vul、Draper VDISC、PrimeVul上，探针性能显著低于SOTA。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明，编码LLM对任意代码的内部表征确实携带漏洞状态信息，激励未来探索轻量级、模型原生的漏洞筛查方法。

Abstract: LLM-based code generation is now embedded in mission-critical pipelines, but defenses against vulnerable output remain post-hoc -- static analyzers, fine-tuned classifiers, or an LLM judge that screen completed code, ignoring the generating model's own internal state. We test a narrower, directly measurable question: when an LLM reads a piece of C/C++ code as context, do its hidden activations already carry a signal about that code's vulnerability status? We extract last prefill token activations from four LLMs (Granite-4.1-8B, Qwen3.5-9B, Qwen3.6-27B, Gemma-4-12B) across three model families and train MLP probes on these activations. We evaluate them on four function-level C/C++ benchmarks (Devign, Big-Vul, Draper VDISC, PrimeVul). Our probes achieve 41.7\% average F1 using 13.4--16.0M-parameter probes -- under 0.2\% of base-model size. On Devign, the best probe (Qwen3.5-9B, 68.8\% F1) matches the published fine-tuned-classifier SOTA (67.9\%) despite reading only a frozen, general-purpose LLM's activations; on the harder, more imbalanced benchmarks (Big-Vul, Draper VDISC, PrimeVul) probes trail SOTA substantially. This is early evidence that a coding LLM's own representation of arbitrary code is informative about that code's vulnerability status, motivating further work toward lightweight, model-native vulnerability screening.

</details>


### [5] [Runtime Governance for Agentic AI: Action-Boundary Control with Trusted Provenance and Fail-Closed Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16891)
*Adam Mazzocchetti*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Aegis运行时治理系统，将模型输出视为动作提议，由可信决策层在工具执行前进行仲裁，在沙盒实验中实现了零风险副作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI系统可执行修改文件、发送消息等工具操作，安全风险从有害文本生成转移到有害操作副作用，而提示级治理无法建立执行边界。

Method: Aegis将模型输出作为动作提议，通过可信决策层依据活动策略评估，服务端解析来源，不确定时默认拒绝，并采用基于法定人数的Senate式结算作为非单边授权路径。

Result: 在6300行数据中，策略条件化产生79条风险泄漏；在2100行Aegis治理行中，零风险工具应用和副作用完成，1832行来源解析正确，1019行Senate结算均满足法定人数和签名证据。

Conclusion: 结果不证明通用智能体安全，但支持在评估沙盒中运行时动作边界治理能防止风险提议成为实际副作用的系统级主张。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems request tool actions that can modify files, send messages, launch jobs, or change workflow state. This shifts the safety problem from harmful text generation to harmful operational side effects. Prompt-level governance can shape model behavior, but it does not create an execution boundary. We introduce Aegis, a runtime governance system that treats model outputs as action proposals and mediates them through a trusted decision layer before tool execution. The model proposes; the trusted runtime decides. Aegis evaluates proposals against active policy state, resolves provenance server-side, fails closed under uncertainty, and routes selected cases through Senate-style settlement, a quorum- based non-unilateral authorization path. We evaluate Aegis on a repeated sandbox corpus spanning five run families, 42 tasks, three conditions, and ten repeats per family. Across 6,300 rows, prompt-policy conditioning produced 79 risky comparator-path leakage rows. Across 2,100 Aegis-governed rows, the system recorded zero governed mock-tool applications and zero governed risky side-effect completions. All 1,832 Aegis-attempted governed rows preserved trusted Aegis-resolved provenance, and all 1,019 Senate-settled rows had quorum and final signed tally evidence. These results do not prove general autonomous-agent safety. They support the narrower systems claim that, in this evaluated sandbox corpus, runtime action-boundary governance prevented observed risky proposals from becoming governed side effects.

</details>


### [6] [Mr.Dec: Daily-Scale Longitudinal Multimodal Modeling for 30-Day Readmission Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16929)
*Minjun Kim,Jong Hak Moon*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Mr.Dec模型，将住院视为每日多模态事件序列，用Transformer解码器整合EHR和胸片数据，结合疾病特异性对比学习，在MIMIC数据集上取得最优结果，并识别出住院期间的“关键日”以支持实时风险分层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测30天再入院的方法常将复杂的纵向病史压缩为固定表征，丢失了逐日的临床信号，无法捕捉患者生理状态随住院时间演变的动态轨迹。

Method: 将每次住院建模为按时间排序的每日多模态事件序列，包括每日EHR更新和间歇性胸片发现，使用Transformer解码器按时间对齐融合；同时引入疾病特异性监督对比学习作为辅助正则化，使潜在空间具备诊断感知结构。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-CXR数据集上达到SOTA性能，验证了保留临床序列完整性的有效性；模型还能识别住院内的“关键日”，提供可操作的临床解释。

Conclusion: 保留每日临床序列的完整性并利用多模态时间融合能显著提升再入院预测，且“关键日”识别为实时风险分层提供了临床可解释的工具。

Abstract: Predicting 30-day hospital readmission is essential for assessing patient stability and optimizing healthcare resources. As clinical risk evolves with the accumulation of evidence during hospitalization, capturing these dynamic trajectories is essential. However, many existing approaches compress the complex longitudinal history into fixed representations, often losing the granular, day-level clinical signals that reflect a patient's evolving physiological state. To address this, we propose Mr.Dec (Multimodal Readmission-risk prediction Decoder), which models each admission as a natural chronological sequence of daily multimodal events. By leveraging a Transformer Decoder, Mr.Dec integrates daily Electronic Health Record(EHR) updates and intermittent Chest X-ray(CXR) findings in a time-aligned stream, reflecting the actual clinical workflow. To ensure robustness, we utilize Disease-Specific Supervised Contrastive Learning as an auxiliary regularization to induce a diagnosis-aware structure in the latent space. Evaluations on the MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-CXR datasets show that Mr.Dec achieves state-of-the-art performance by preserving the integrity of the clinical sequence. Furthermore, our model identifies "Critical Days" within an admission, providing actionable and clinically grounded interpretations for real-time risk stratification. Code is available at: https://github.com/yejix-ai/MR.DEC

</details>


### [7] [Remote-Timer-as-a-Service: Efficient Microarchitectural Leakage in the Cloud with Remote Timers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17043)
*Martin Schwarzl,Haocheng Xiao,Albert Pedersen,Sam Ainsworth,Nigel Topham*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 通过微架构放大和远程计时技术，在Cloudflare Workers生产环境中实现了远程Spectre攻击，以99.16%的准确率泄露同租户JWT令牌，速率提升至12 bit/s，并促使官方集成V8沙箱、增强DyPrIs检测及部署MPK隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cloudflare Workers依赖语言级隔离并移除进程边界以降低延迟，但该架构面临Spectre攻击风险；现有DyPrIs和定时器限制在真实生产环境中的防护效果尚未被充分验证，需测试其实际安全性。

Method: 采用微架构时间放大技术提升计时分辨率，结合远程计时器绕过定时器冻结/粗化限制；构造跨租户的瞬态执行攻击链，在Cloudflare Workers生产环境内从同驻受害Worker中提取JWT令牌，并优化攻击吞吐与精度。

Result: 攻击速率从2 bit/min提升至12 bit/s，令牌恢复准确率达99.16%，证明现有DyPrIs与计时限制无法有效阻止实际Spectre利用；泄露数据直接威胁用户凭据安全。

Conclusion: 生产级语言隔离需结合硬件辅助防护；仅依赖软件计时限制和动态进程隔离不足以抵御微架构攻击，必须采用V8沙箱、MPK内存隔离及强化检测机制协同防御。

Abstract: Edge computing solutions have become a crucial part of the industry, delivering fast, flexible and scalable applications close to the end users, with typical use cases including dynamic content creation, image resizing and chatbots. Cloudflare Workers is one such framework, which handles millions of HTTP requests per second worldwide. To reduce start-up latency, Cloudflare Workers removes process-isolation boundaries between multiple tenants and leverages language-level isolation. This architecture poses the risk of Spectre attacks. To mitigate these, Cloudflare Workers previously introduced several countermeasures such as restricted timer measurements, no shared memory, no multithreading and Dynamic Process Isolation (DyPrIs), detecting potential attacks and process-isolating potentially malicious scripts.
  We demonstrate that the production implementation of DyPrIs was insufficient. We adopt microarchitectural amplification techniques and discover various possibilities to measure time in the production environment of Cloudflare Workers. Given these techniques, we show that freezing and coarsening timers in the Cloudflare Workers security model is insufficient. Leveraging both timing amplification and remote timers, we demonstrate a remote Spectre attack that leaks a JWT token from a co-located victim worker in the Cloudflare Workers production environment. We outperform the existing attack by orders of magnitude, going from 2 bit/min to up to 12 bit/s at an accuracy of 99.16%, posing an immediate risk to customer data. Following our end-to-end attack, Cloudflare Workers mitigated it in a coordinated effort by integrating the V8 Sandbox limiting transient access to 64-bit pointers, improving the detection capabilities of DyPrIs, and deploying hardware-assisted MPK-based in-process isolation to confine each tenant heap under a dedicated memory-protection key.

</details>


### [8] [CUSTOS: Toward Forensic-Ready Zero Trust at the Capture-Containment Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17068)
*Avinash Srinivasan,John Paramadilok*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出CUSTOS取证就绪零信任参考架构，通过FMP实现持续取证与响应，在决策记录、事件响应和遥测方面取得权衡，并揭示易失性证据不可恢复的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零信任机制虽提升安全性，但削弱了负载可见性、IP溯源性，并缩短了易失性证据获取窗口，导致取证困难。

Method: 设计以法证管理点（FMP）为核心的参考架构，集成分层捕获、身份/策略关联重构、遥测编排与受控调查访问；通过网关、运行时机理和编排器实验评估组件级原型。

Result: 决策记录在进程内策略引擎上仅产生1.9-3.0%吞吐开销；FMP屏障后排序SIGKILL可实现100%秘密恢复（对比并发捕获0%）；身份导向遥测对决策模式覆盖64-75%（网络导向仅18-30%）。

Conclusion: 取证就绪零信任需持续证据底座与有界响应捕获并存，同时明确在某些对抗或未排序场景下易失性证据仍无法恢复。

Abstract: Zero Trust (ZT) replaces implicit trust with continuous verification, but mutual TLS, ephemeral workloads, identity-centric control, and automated remediation reduce payload visibility, weaken IP-based attribution, and shrink the window for acquiring volatile evidence. We propose CUSTOS, a forensic-ready ZT reference architecture centered on a Forensic Management Point (FMP) that coordinates tiered capture, identity- and policy-linked reconstruction, telemetry orchestration, and ZT-controlled investigative access. We evaluate a composed, component-level prototype using a live enforcement gateway plus separate runtime and orchestrator experiments. An always-on decision record is captured and hash-chained on the gateway at a 1.9-3.0\% throughput cost on in-process policy engines, preserving decision provenance outside the monitored workload under stated trust assumptions. Reactive checkpointing (about 65 ms) precedes seconds-scale defender-routed eviction but loses to unsequenced direct SIGKILL (about 9 ms), in-kernel enforcement, and adversarial self-destruction, producing the forensic shredder effect. On a real container, concurrent capture and SIGKILL recovered the planted secret in 0/1000 trials; sequencing SIGKILL behind the FMP barrier recovered it in 1000/1000. The primary integrated single-node Kubernetes race checkpoints an FMP-controlled process; container-memory capture is evaluated separately and was unavailable in the managed-Kubernetes configuration. Across five public benchmark datasets and a synthetic schema reference, identity-oriented telemetry populates 64-75\% of the decision-record schema against 18-30\% for network-oriented, while rate limiting bounds the full-memory admission ceiling. These results show that forensic-ready ZT requires both an always-on evidentiary floor and bounded reactive capture, while identifying where volatile evidence remains unrecoverable.

</details>


### [9] [SW-ProxyCE: Zero-Query Adversarial Transfer from Public EEG Encoders to Private Downstream Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16931)
*Linhua Cong,Dingkun Liu,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文首次研究EEG基础模型在公开编码器-私有下游模型场景下的对抗迁移攻击，提出SW-ProxyCE方法，无需查询即可利用少量带标签参考数据生成可迁移对抗样本，在多个任务和编码器上验证了攻击有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型公开编码器带来安全风险：攻击者可利用公开表示攻击私有下游模型，而现有研究未探索此类威胁。

Method: 提出SW-ProxyCE框架，通过收缩白化类原型从少量参考集恢复任务决策几何，无需训练替代分类器即可生成迁移对抗样本。

Conclusion: EEG基础模型的公开编码器存在可利用的对抗漏洞，需在设计部署时考虑安全防护。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for EEG decoding by learning reusable representations from large-scale heterogeneous neural recordings. However, the open release of EEG foundation encoders, while facilitating downstream developments, also introduces a previously unexplored security risk: publicly available representations may make private downstream models vulnerable. This paper investigates adversarial transfer attacks in EEG foundation model deployment in a public-encoder and private-downstream setting, where attackers have white-box access to a released encoder and a small task-matched labeled reference set, but no access or query to victim parameters, outputs, or gradients. We propose Shrinkage-Whitened Proxy Cross-Entropy (SW-ProxyCE), a query-free task-aware attack framework that recovers task-level decision geometry from a small labeled reference set through shrinkage-whitened class prototypes, enabling transferable adversarial generation without training an additional surrogate classifier. We evaluated SW-ProxyCE across three EEG tasks using three general-purpose foundation encoders and a paradigm-specific pre-trained encoder, covering both linear-probing and full-fine-tuning downstream models in cross-subject and within-subject scenarios. Results demonstrated that adversarial examples generated from the public encoder and limited labeled references can effectively transfer to inaccessible downstream models. SW-ProxyCE consistently outperformed task-agnostic representation-shift attacks, revealing that the strong transferability of EEG foundation models does not necessarily lead to adversarial robustness. Our code will be available on GitHub.

</details>

*Sandesh More,Elton Batista,Karla Daley,Sneha Sudhakaran*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: SentryBus通过监测I2C传感器总线的事务时序、读写序列、长度、地址行为及寄存器状态等特征，在主机侧被动检测传感器接口的中间人攻击，实验表明内联拦截器可被限制在采集服务时间的0.842%内，但数据转换特征不具备跨会话可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗物联网、无人机及信息物理系统通常信任到达处理器的传感器测量值，而攻击者可利用数字接口注入合法固件接受的虚假数据，现有系统缺乏针对此类篡改的检测手段。

Method: 在I2C传感器总线上建模采集行为特征（事务时序、读写序列、传输长度、地址行为、寄存器/FIFO状态访问及原始数据转换），模拟内联拦截器、并行控制器、传感器替换或主机入侵等攻击模型，并构建双端测试平台，其中检测器仅使用面向主机的总线数据，其余视角作为地面真值。

Result: 生理数据场景中，内联拦截器被限制在采集服务时间的0.842%内且不干扰采集调度和有效载荷；清洁采集在6304秒内保持稳定且无时钟回归；数据转换特征编码会话特定信号统计量，无法跨会话复用；低成本分析仪可截断捕获而不引发内核可见错误。

Conclusion: 被动主机侧监测在界定传感器接口篡改的可区分边界上具有潜力，但数据特征缺乏跨会话泛化性，且因攻击试验尚未完成，目前无法声称检测率。

Abstract: Sensor-driven systems in medical Internet of Things devices, drones, and cyber-physical systems commonly trust a measurement once it reaches the embedded processor. An adversary on the digital interface between sensor and processor can supply a plausible value that correct firmware accepts and reports as ordinary telemetry. The hypothesis is that sensor interface manipulation leaves observable evidence on the acquisition path, that the evidence appears at different vantages depending on attacker position, and that a passive host-side monitor therefore has a measurable boundary beyond which manipulation becomes indistinguishable from legitimate acquisition. SentryBus models acquisition behavior on the I2C sensor bus using transaction timing, read and write sequences, transfer lengths, address behavior, register and FIFO state access, and raw data transitions. The adversary is modeled as an inline interposer, parallel controller, sensor replacement, or compromised host, because a commodity target-only sensor cannot initiate transfers or stretch, reorder, or delay bus transactions. A dual sided testbed captures both busses, host memory, and telemetry, and the detector consumes the host facing bus alone while the remaining vantages serve as ground truth. A physiological instantiation reports three measured results: an inline interposer bounded at 0.842 percent of acquisition service time while preserving acquisition schedule and payload content, clean acquisition stability sustained over 6304 seconds at the telemetry vantage with no clock regression, and a negative result establishing that data-transition features encode session specific signal statistics and do not transfer across capture sessions. Instrument characterization shows that a low-cost analyzer can truncate captures without kernel visible error. Controlled attack trials are still outstanding, so no detection rate is claimed.

</details>


### [11] [Structured Driving-State Narratives for Small Language Model-Based GNSS Spoofing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17092)
*Abyad Enan,Sagar Dasgupta,Mizanur Rahman,Mashrur Chowdhury*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于小语言模型（SLM）的框架，用于检测和分类自动驾驶车辆中的GNSS欺骗攻击，通过将GNSS与其他传感源的驾驶状态转换为语义叙述供SLM分析，在精度上与LLM相当（准确率96.99%），但计算资源需求更低，适合车载平台实时部署。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶依赖GNSS定位，但欺骗信号可导致错误车辆状态，现有检测方法可能计算开销大或不适合资源受限环境，需开发高效且准确的实时检测方案。

Method: 将GNSS和其他传感源独立得到的驾驶行为转换为结构化语义叙述，输入SLM进行欺骗检测与攻击类型分类（五类），并与在相同数据上微调的LLM对比，评估指标包括精度、召回率、F1分数、推理延迟和GPU内存。

Result: SLM达到平均准确率96.99%、精确率99.05%、召回率95.59%、F1分数97.18%，性能与LLM相当，但推理延迟更低、GPU内存占用更少，且在异地实地数据（南卡罗来纳州）上验证有效。

Conclusion: 该SLM框架能实时检测和分类GNSS欺骗攻击，计算和内存需求低，适合部署于资源受限的车辆计算平台，为自动驾驶安全提供高效保障。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) depend on reliable Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) positioning. However, spoofed GNSS signals can induce plausible but incorrect vehicle states. This study develops a small language model (SLM)-based framework for detecting and classifying GNSS spoofing attacks by comparing vehicle behaviors independently derived from GNSS and other sensing sources. The framework converts independent driving states from GNSS and other sensing sources into structured semantic narratives that are provided to an SLM for spoofing detection and attack classification. The performance of the SLM-based framework is compared with large language models (LLMs) fine-tuned on identical training data and evaluated on the same test set. The evaluation considers five classes: no attack, overshoot attack, stopped attack, turn-by-turn attack, and wrong-turn attack. The framework is also evaluated with geographically unseen field data collected in Clemson, South Carolina, United States. Experimental results indicate that the evaluated SLMs achieve performance similar to the LLMs, achieving an average accuracy of 96.99%, precision of 99.05%, recall of 95.59%, and F1-score of 97.18%. In terms of computational efficiency and resource utilization, the SLMs demonstrate advantages over the LLMs by requiring lower inference latency and less GPU memory during both fine-tuning and inference. Evaluation using field data collected in a geographically distinct location further demonstrated its efficacy. The presented framework can detect and classify GNSS spoofing attacks in real-time while requiring relatively low computational and memory resources, and is therefore suitable for deployment on resource-constrained vehicular computing platforms.

</details>


### [12] [SkillEffect: Checked Lowering for Memory-Bounded Agent Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17007)
*Yinuo Wang,Yiyu Shi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出SkillEffect，一种检查降级运行时，用于在代理工具调用前强制执行异构内存关系，通过独立检查器验证代码和插件契约，减少峰值内存并提高任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型将代理技能转化为程序时，即使语义正确也可能因加载整个输入而超出工具调用的内存限制，需要一种机制在授权执行前确保内存安全。

Method: SkillEffect采用检查降级架构，每个关系插件提供源识别器、输入事实提取器、有界IR构造器、竞技场边界函数和后置条件；共享运行时负责检查选择、有界VM执行、原子容量租赁和分阶段发布。

Result: 在六个算子族中，有界访问显著降低峰值内存并在固定上限下提高完成率；六个插件覆盖五种执行模式，检查器接受所有合法配置并拒绝所有对抗性提案。

Conclusion: 单一检查降级架构能够对代理工具调度强制执行异构注册内存关系，且新关系和新保留状态模式可复用相同信任边界。

Abstract: Agent Skills can specify procedural and resource obligations for tool use, and language models instantiate them as concrete programs. However, when models turn this guidance into code for existing tool interfaces, even a semantically correct program may load an entire input and exceed the memory available to one tool call. We present SkillEffect, a checked-lowering runtime for computations with a recoverable source relation, an audited bounded implementation, and a registered output postcondition. Before granting execution authority, an independent checker rebuilds each proposed lowering from the submitted program and immutable input. Every relation plugin supplies a source recognizer, input-fact extractor, bounded-IR constructor, arena-bound function, and postcondition; one common runtime provides checked selection, bounded-VM execution, atomic capacity leasing, and staged publication. Generality in SkillEffect is architectural rather than automatic: each supported computation requires an audited relation plugin, while the dispatch, resource-control, execution, and publication mechanisms are shared across plugins. Across six operator families, bounded access substantially reduces peak memory and improves completion under externally fixed caps. Six plugins instantiate the same contract across five execution patterns, from streaming reduction to bounded-heap Top-k. The XLSX onboarding study and Top-k extension show that a new relation and a new retained-state pattern reuse the same trust boundary, while the checker accepts all evaluated legal configurations and rejects all adversarial proposals. Together, these results show that one checked-lowering architecture can enforce heterogeneous registered memory relations at Agent tool dispatch.

</details>


### [13] [Digital Twin-Based Intrusion Detection for Vehicle Powertrain CAN Bus Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17093)
*Araf Rahman,M Sabbir Salek,Mashrur Chowdhury*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种基于数字孪生（DT）的汽车CAN总线入侵检测系统，通过LSTM联合建模物理信号关系，检测保持正常通信模式的隐蔽载荷攻击，在真实数据上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAN总线IDS主要检测报文时序、频率或序列异常，无法识别保持这些属性但篡改载荷的攻击；数字孪生虽用于仿真和攻击生成，但尚未用于入侵检测。

Method: 训练共享编码器LSTM数字孪生，联合预测7个数值和2个分类齿轮信号（共17个解码信号，24步窗口），通过残差阈值标记时间步，并采用自适应回滚防止持续污染影响输入历史。

Result: 在四种攻击（平台、连续漂移、伪装、齿轮伪装）下，DT检测率显著优于基线的范围和合理性方法，连续漂移达94.6%、伪装达89.2%，但误报率高达39.6%。

Conclusion: 学习耦合车辆动力学可有效检测保持正常CAN通信模式的隐蔽载荷攻击，支持基于行为的车联网安全，但需提升鲁棒性以降低高误报率。

Abstract: Existing automotive intrusion detection systems (IDSs) for the Controller Area Network (CAN) largely target discrepancies in message timing, frequency, or sequencing and cannot detect attacks that preserve these properties while manipulating the payload. Digital twins (DTs) have been used to emulate CAN traffic and generate attack scenarios for IDS evaluation, but their use for intrusion detection remains unexplored. This study develops a DT-based IDS that jointly models physical relationships among decoded powertrain signals and identifies attacks through residuals between predicted and observed behavior. A shared-encoder LSTM DT was trained on 17 decoded signals from a real Hyundai/Kia CAN log to jointly predict seven numeric and two categorical gear signals over a 24-step window. A timestep is flagged when a residual exceeds a calibrated threshold, while adaptive rollout protects the twin's input history from sustained contamination. Four attacks (plateau, continuous drift, masquerade, and gear masquerade) were evaluated against the twin and a range-and-plausibility baseline. The DT outperformed the baseline across all attacks, achieving detection rates of 94.6% for continuous drift and 89.2% for masquerade, while the baseline detected almost none of the fabricated payload attacks. These results demonstrate that learning coupled vehicle dynamics enables detection of stealthy payload manipulations that preserve normal CAN communication patterns. False positive rates reached 39.6%, highlighting the need for improved robustness under sustained attacks. The DT-based IDS shows promise for detecting stealthy payload-level CAN attacks that preserve normal communication patterns, supporting behavior-based cybersecurity for connected and automated vehicles.

</details>


### [14] [RoBell-RVFL: A Robust Generalized Bell Random Vector Functional Link Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16965)
*A. Rahaman,A. Quadir,M. Tanveer*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出RoBell-RVFL，一种鲁棒且轻量的质量感知广义钟形随机向量功能链接网络，通过双策略样本级加权机制（少数类单位权重、多数类基于gbell隶属度的自适应加权）在核诱导特征空间中抑制噪声、边界和离群样本，在UCI/KEEL基准及高达40%标签噪声下显著优于现有RVFL变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类别不平衡处理方法（如SMOTE、类加权损失）只关注类别比例而忽略类内分布，对标签噪声和离群点脆弱；随机神经网络在真实数据中易受多数类主导而偏向多数类，忽视关键少数类样本。

Method: RoBell-RVFL采用双策略：少数类样本使用单位权重严格保留信息；多数类样本通过概率加权广义钟形（gbell）隶属函数在核诱导特征空间中自适应调节权重，结合局部类别概率和分布信息，在保持RVFL闭式解效率的同时实现样本级质量感知控制。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL数据集上的广泛评估以及高达40%标签噪声的鲁棒性测试表明，RoBell-RVFL一致且显著优于近期最先进的RVFL变体。

Conclusion: 自适应、质量感知的样本加权对于鲁棒RVFL学习至关重要；传统的全局加权方案在噪声和不平衡环境中效果不佳，而RoBell-RVFL证明了融入局部类别信息的样本级权重调节能有效提升模型鲁棒性和不平衡学习性能。

Abstract: The dominance of majority classes in real-world datasets poses a fundamental challenge to randomized neural networks, often biasing decision boundaries and overlooking critical minority samples. Existing remedies, such as synthetic minority over-sampling (SMOTE) and class-weighted loss functions, primarily address class proportions while neglecting intra-class distribution, making them vulnerable to label noise and outliers. In this paper, we propose \textbf{RoBell-RVFL}, a robust and lightweight \emph{quality-aware} generalized bell random vector functional link network that redefines how randomized models handle class imbalance and noisy data. RoBell-RVFL employs a dual-strategy, sample-level weighting mechanism that strictly preserves minority class information using unit weights, while adaptively regulating the influence of majority class samples through a probability-weighted generalized bell (gbell) membership function in a kernel-induced feature space. This design effectively suppresses noisy, boundary, and outlier samples within the majority class, enabling the network to learn from informative samples rather than merely abundant ones. By explicitly incorporating local class probability and class distribution information into the learning process, RoBell-RVFL achieves adaptive control over sample contributions without sacrificing the closed-form learning efficiency of RVFL networks. Extensive evaluations on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets, along with robustness tests under up to 40\% label noise, demonstrate that RoBell-RVFL consistently and significantly outperforms recent state-of-the-art RVFL variants. The results indicate that adaptive, quality-aware sample weighting is essential for robust RVFL learning, rendering conventional global weighting schemes ineffective in noisy and imbalanced environments.

</details>


### [15] [DiSCO: Defending text-to-image generation through distribution-guided contrastive prompt optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17067)
*Tong Zhang,Motasem Alfarra,Carlos Hinojosa,Christos Louizos,Bernard Ghanem*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出DiSCO，一种零样本、纯黑盒的提示词级防御方法，通过分布引导的后缀扩展与对比评分，在不修改模型的情况下降低文本到图像模型生成不安全内容的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御多依赖白盒假设，无法适用于专有模型；基于LLM提示词重写的黑盒方法在'良性对抗'场景下失效，即提示词语义安全但仍会触发有害生成。

Method: DiSCO在提示词层面进行束搜索式的后缀扩展，利用目标模型自身生成的safe/unsafe图像池进行对比评分优化，并通过迭代自适应反馈直至生成安全内容。

Result: 在I2P基准上，对未防御和已防御模型分别实现37.7%和25.13%的ASR降低，同时保持语义保真并提升图像一致性。

Conclusion: DiSCO作为即插即用、架构无关的黑盒模块，可便捷应用于任何文本到图像系统，无需改动模型内部。

Abstract: As text-to-image generative models advance, they raise critical safety concerns, particularly the generation of Not-Safe-For-Work (NSFW) content such as violence and nudity, further exacerbated by red-teaming adversarial attacks. Existing defenses predominantly operate under white-box assumptions, relying on text encoder optimization, weight editing, or inference-time intervention, and fundamentally cannot scale to proprietary models. Black-box alternatives based on LLM prompt rewriting offer broader applicability, yet fail in a critical regime we identify as the \textit{benign adversarial} problem: prompts that are linguistically safe but still trigger harmful generation due to the model's learned data distribution. We propose DiSCO, a zero-shot, strictly black-box defense that operates entirely at the prompt level as a plug-and-play module, requiring no model retraining, fine-tuning, or access to model internals. DiSCO performs distribution-guided suffix expansion via beam search, optimized through contrastive scoring over safe and unsafe image pools generated by the target model itself, with iterative adaptive feedback until safe content is produced. We demonstrate that DiSCO consistently enhances the safety of both undefended and defended models on the I2P benchmark under multiple red-teaming attacks, achieving 37.7% and 25.13% ASR reduction, respectively, while maintaining semantic fidelity and improving image coherence. As a black-box, architecture-agnostic module, DiSCO can be readily applied to any text-to-image system without necessitating any changes to the model itself.

</details>


### [16] [Authorization Before Context: A Model-Neutral Audience Boundary Against Cross-Audience Memory Leakage in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17148)
*Sibo Liu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种在记忆到上下文转换时应用的、基于反单调受众成员规则的授权边界，确保任何事实不得泄露给未授权观众，并在合成数据集上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人语言代理可能将从某受众处学到的事实错误地放入为另一受众生成的上下文中，形成攻击面；现有方法依赖模型行为，缺乏在组装上下文时的明确授权控制。

Method: 在记忆到上下文转换时引入单一授权规则：每条记忆携带其记录时的受众，当前观众集合从通道元数据读取（模糊时回退为公开），仅当所有当前观众均属于原受众时才允许该记忆进入上下文。通过排除而非模型行为保证狭窄受众信息不流向更广受众，且中毒记忆无法扩大自身受众。

Result: 在合成的Contextual-Integrity测试集上，所提边界未让任何被禁止的事实进入上下文，而未加范围的基线方法在构造上就包含此类事实；同时审计所有读取路径均失败关闭。

Conclusion: 该授权边界是模型无关的上下文组装不变量，能有效阻断跨受众信息泄露；但证据为初步且合成的，仍需更广泛的验证。

Abstract: A personal language agent learns a fact from one audience and may later place it in the prompt it assembles for another. This memory-to-context step is an attack surface: ambiguous or inconsistent channels, cross-audience prying, and poisoned memory can each cause the system to assemble context containing a fact relevant to the query yet unauthorized for the current viewers. We introduce authorization before context: a single, anti-monotone audience-membership rule applied at the memory-to-context transition. Each item carries the audience present when it was recorded; the current viewer set is read from channel metadata and falls back to public when ambiguous; and the item is admitted only when every current viewer already belonged to its audience. We prove that this rule gives every participant cross-channel recall while ensuring, by exclusion rather than by model behavior, that nothing recorded for a narrower audience reaches a broader one and that poisoned memory cannot widen its own audience. The boundary is a model-neutral invariant on the exact assembled context: a forbidden fact must be absent before the model is called. On a synthetic Contextual-Integrity suite, no forbidden fact entered the context our boundary assembled, whereas unscoped baselines included such facts by construction; we further audit that every read path fails closed. The evidence is preliminary and synthetic.

</details>


### [17] [Beyond the Hype: Evaluating LLM Integration and Practical Limitations in Security Operation Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17154)
*Elnaz Rabieinejad,Ali Dehghantanha,Fattane Zarrinkalam,Sarina Dastgerdy*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 通过对20名SOC从业者的半结构化访谈，发现LLM在低风险、可快速验证的任务中能节省时间，但被视为初步草稿而非决策级结论；高风险决策中因输出不可靠和推理不透明而信任有限，主要依赖临时验证和人工监督，缺乏标准化缓解措施。基于此提出LLM集成成熟度模型，并规划强调可审计性与透明解释机制的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在SOC中的文本分析工作（如警报情境化、事件摘要、调查文档起草）中应用日益增多，但从业者普遍担忧幻觉、推理不透明及验证负担等关键运营问题，而现有研究缺乏对实际从业者经验的系统深入理解，难以指导安全采用。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈法，对20名SOC从业者（包括一线分析师、SOC经理和工具开发者）进行定性研究，收集其关于LLM使用体验、信任、验证实践及部署障碍的一手叙述，并基于访谈结果设计成熟度评估框架。

Result: 访谈显示，参与者感知到低风险任务（如日志摘要、起草初始调查线索）的时间节省收益，但普遍将LLM输出定性为初步草稿或建议；对高风险安全决策的信任有限，原因在于输出可靠性不足和推理过程难解；验证依赖非正式临时规范和持续人工监督，而非标准化流程。

Conclusion: LLM在SOC中的价值目前局限于低风险辅助角色，安全采用需弥补标准化验证缺口。研究提出两条路径：一是开发成熟度模型以评估组织集成准备度，二是建立研究议程，聚焦可审计性和透明解释机制，从而在保障安全性的前提下促进LLM负责任落地。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being explored within Security Operation Centers (SOCs) to support text-heavy analytical work such as alert contextualization, incident summarization, and drafting investigative artifacts. Despite this interest, practitioners describe critical operational concerns, most notably hallucinations (plausible but incorrect outputs), opaque reasoning, and the verification effort required to safely use model-generated content in security workflows. In this paper, we present findings from semi-structured interviews with 20 SOC practitioners spanning frontline analysts, SOC managers, and tool developers. Participants report perceived time savings for low-stakes tasks that are quickly verifiable (e.g., summarizing logs or drafting initial investigative leads), but they consistently frame LLM outputs as preliminary drafts and suggestions rather than decision-grade conclusions. Participants also describe limited trust in LLMs for high-stakes security decisions due to unreliable outputs and unclear model reasoning, and they report relying primarily on ad-hoc verification norms and continuous human oversight rather than standardized mitigation procedures. Based on these interview-grounded accounts, we introduce a maturity rubric to characterize readiness for LLM integration and outline a research agenda emphasizing auditability and transparent explanation mechanisms to support safer adoption in SOC workflows.

</details>

*Youwei Zhong,Ben Merbaum,Timos Antonopoulos,Ning Luo,Charalampos Papamanthou,Katerina Sotiraki,Ruzica Piskac*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: PANDA 是一个基于零知识证明的可扩展系统，能在不泄露模型参数的前提下证明神经网络的鲁棒性和公平性，其核心创新是面向非线性激活层的线性松弛界限证明算法，支持比现有方法大4个数量级的网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型的鲁棒性和公平性需要形式化保证，但模型参数常为商业机密，无法向审计者或终端用户披露，因此需要一种既能验证性质又不泄露参数的技术。

Method: 基于 CROWN 鲁棒性认证框架，提出针对非线性激活层线性松弛界限的新颖证明算法，生成轻量级证据，并结合零知识证明实现参数隐藏，复杂度随神经元数量多项式增长。

Result: 对超过290万参数的神经网络，5分钟内生成局部鲁棒性证明，10秒内完成验证；支持网络规模比先前方法大4个数量级，且证明者开销显著降低。

Conclusion: PANDA 实现了首个可扩展的基于 ZKP 的神经网络鲁棒性证明系统，克服了先前指数级算法的局限性，为安全关键和合规场景提供了实用方案。

Abstract: With the growing deployment of machine learning models, formal guarantees of the robustness and fairness of these models have become increasingly important in safety-critical and legal-compliance settings. However, model parameters are often commercial secrets that cannot be disclosed to auditors or end users. To this end, we present PANDA, a scalable system that uses zero-knowledge proofs (ZKPs) to prove the robustness and fairness properties of a model without revealing its private parameters. PANDA is built on top of CROWN, an efficient robustness certification framework that is used in many state-of-the-art formal verification tools for neural networks. The core contribution of PANDA is a novel algorithm for proving linear relaxation bounds for non-linear activation layers, yielding simple, lightweight proofs. Remarkably, our system can generate proofs of local robustness for neural networks with more than 2.9M parameters in 5 minutes, and can verify them in 10 seconds. Prior ZKP-based robustness system rely on exponential-time algorithms that cannot scale to nontrivial networks. In contrast, PANDA scales polynomially in the number of neurons in a network, allowing us to support neural networks 4 orders of magnitude larger than previous approaches with significantly reduced prover overhead.

</details>


### [19] [PACE: Policy-Attested Contract Execution for Safe AI Agents in Decentralized Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17220)
*Rabimba Karanjai,Yang Lu,Richard Williamson,Hemanth Hm,Prakhar Mehrotra,Lei Xu,Weidong,Shi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: PACE是一个交易级授权框架，通过类型化交易意图、确定性策略验证器和签名策略决策记录（PDR），将LLM代理的批准与链上精确执行字节绑定，在确定性沙箱中实现0.00不安全执行率和0.00误报率，对比无防护基线0.80，额外开销仅29,826-31,822 gas。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的DeFi代理易受提示注入影响，且缺乏将验证者批准绑定到实际提交交易字节的机制，导致不安全执行风险。

Method: 在LLM代理与链上执行之间插入策略验证层，引入类型化交易意图、确定性策略验证器及签名PDR，通过Solidity智能账户在链上强制验证，并包含回放和过期保护。

Result: 在40个任务、4类攻击加良性场景、2,800次试验中，PACE实现0.00不安全率和0.00误报率；消融研究确定宽松策略设置（+57.5个百分点）和合约白名单（+12.5个百分点）为主要安全组件；gas开销为29,826-31,822。

Conclusion: PACE在可复现基准中达到逻辑级安全性，但作者明确声明其并非部署级DeFi安全方案；辅助的实时LLM评估和主网分叉测试仅作补充，不替代确定性基准结论。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents are emerging as interfaces for decentralized finance (DeFi) actions such as swaps, lending operations, and yield management. Because these agents rely on large language models (LLMs) to plan transactions, they inherit the LLM's susceptibility to prompt injection and lack of mechanisms to bind a verifier's approval to the exact transaction ultimately submitted on-chain. We present PACE (Policy-Attested Contract Execution), a transaction-level authorization framework that interposes between an LLM-based agent and on-chain execution. PACE introduces typed transaction intents, a deterministic policy verifier, and signed Policy Decision Records (PDRs) that cryptographically bind the approved intent, policy, and simulation report to the exact execution bytes, with replay and expiration protection. A Solidity smart account enforces PDR signatures on-chain with a measured overhead of 29,826-31,822 gas. We evaluate PACE against six baselines on 40 tasks spanning four attack categories plus benign utility (2,800 trials, 10 seeds). In our deterministic sandbox, PACE achieves a 0.00 unsafe execution rate and 0.00 false-positive rate on benign tasks, compared to 0.80 for the unguarded baseline. Ablation studies identify permissive policy settings (+57.5 pp) and the touched-contract allowlist (+12.5 pp) as the dominant safety components. To test whether the same deterministic floor holds for real model outputs, the artifact additionally provides a three-model live-LLM evaluation over the full task suite with repeated runs. A mainnet-fork harness is included for archive-RPC deployments, but fork results are reported only when the corresponding artifacts are generated. These auxiliary studies are separate from, and never substitute for, the deterministic benchmark. We frame our claims as logic-level safety within a reproducible benchmark rather than deployment-ready DeFi security.

</details>


### [20] [COMIC: Reference-Aware Safety Gating for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17234)
*Md Abdullahil Oaphy,Anhao Xiang,Zongxing Xie,Huayue Gu,Chenyu Wang,Honghui Xu*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出多模态大语言模型中的引用依赖型越狱漏洞，并设计引用感知的安全门控机制COMIC，通过显式建模操作-目标配对来提升防御鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态防御将提示-图像对整体审核，但真正危险的是模型将良性操作（如摘要、翻译）绑定到特定视觉目标后才产生的不安全行为，这种引用依赖型失败模式未被现有防御覆盖。

Method: COMIC先推断用户请求的操作和引用类型，结合OCR与开放词汇提议构建候选目标，在生成前对操作-目标对进行安全评估，并通过最大风险聚合与质量感知路由实现保守决策。

Result: 在多个开源MLLM及本地化/广泛越狱基准上，COMIC一致提升鲁棒性，同时保持良性任务效用和实际效率。

Conclusion: 多模态安全必须显式建模请求操作、视觉目标及接地置信度，否则无法可靠防御引用依赖型攻击。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interact with screenshots, scanned documents, diagrams, and other visually grounded inputs. This shift introduces a new safety risk: in many multimodal jailbreaks, neither the prompt nor the image is harmful in isolation. Unsafe behavior emerges only when the model binds an apparently benign operation, such as summarizing, translating, or following, to a localized visual target. This reveals a structural weakness in current multimodal defenses, which largely moderate the prompt-image pair as a whole even though the true security-relevant unit is the grounded operation-target pair produced during dereference. In this work, we identify and analyze this reference-dependent failure mode and show that existing defenses degrade when harmful semantics are localized, activated only after grounding, and dependent on visual reference resolution. To address this problem, we propose COMIC (Context-Operation-Modality-Image-Classifier), a reference-aware pre-generation safety gate for MLLMs. COMIC first infers the requested operation and reference type, constructs candidate targets from OCR and open-vocabulary proposals, grounds plausible referents, and evaluates safety over explicit operation-target pairs. To handle ambiguity conservatively, COMIC combines max-risk aggregation with quality-aware routing before deciding whether to forward or block a request. We evaluate COMIC across multiple open-source MLLMs, localized and broader multimodal jailbreak benchmarks, and benign reference-sensitive settings. The results show that COMIC consistently improves robustness while preserving benign utility and practical efficiency. More broadly, our findings suggest that multimodal safety cannot be enforced reliably without modeling the requested operation, the visual target to which it applies, and the confidence of that grounding.

</details>


### [21] [Benchmarking the Benchmarks: Evaluating Automated Safety Benchmarks for Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17183)
*Nyamtulla Shaik,Fengjun Li,Bo Luo*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 通过统一评判标准评估26个开源小语言模型在5个基准套件上的表现，发现大量模糊评判结果，表明面向大语言模型的安全基准不足以单独评估小语言模型的安全性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型在资源受限和隐私敏感场景中部署日益增多，但现有安全/偏见基准专为大型语言模型设计，可能无法可靠迁移到小语言模型上，因此需验证这些基准的有效性和稳健性。

Method: 在统一评判规则（有害=0、安全=1、模糊/无关=0.5）下，对26个开源小语言模型和5个常用基准套件进行大规模评估，并分析模糊率与文本特征（词汇密度、困惑度、输出长度、自洽性等）及模型架构的关系，同时测试聚合排名的数学稳定性。

Result: 模糊评判占据主导，且与提示复杂度和模型架构相关；模糊率随词汇密度、输出困惑度和长度增加而上升，随词汇复杂度、自洽性和回复-提示相似性下降；均值排名在合理模糊处理下发生显著变化。

Conclusion: 大语言模型安全基准单独不足以作为小语言模型安全评估的证据，存在能力-安全混淆问题，且聚合榜单对模糊处理方式高度敏感，需发展专门针对小语言模型的可靠评估方法。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) are increasingly deployed in resource-constrained, privacy-sensitive settings, where safety and bias failures can cause security and societal risks. However, existing AI safety\slash security\slash compliance benchmarks are designed for large language models that may not transfer reliably to SLMs. We therefore ask: Can these benchmarks effectively and reliably evaluate SLMs? To answer this question, we conduct a large-scale assessment of the effectiveness and robustness of these automated pipelines by evaluating five widely used benchmark suites across 26 open-source SLMs under a unified judging rubric, which assigns a score of 0, 1, or 0.5 to harmful, safe, or ambiguous/irrelevant responses, respectively. Across the benchmarks, ambiguous judgments dominate and correlate with prompt complexity and model architecture, indicating that {\em LLM-centric safety benchmarks are insufficient as standalone evidence for SLM safety assessment}. In general, the ambiguity rate increases with lexical density, output perplexity, and output length and decreases with lexical sophistication, self-coherence, and reply-prompt similarity. This reveals a capability-safety confound that mixes model capability with apparent safety. Since ambiguity is prevalent, aggregate mean-score leaderboards are mathematically brittle: model rankings change significantly under reasonable ambiguity treatments, even when the underlying outputs remain unchanged.

</details>


### [22] [ADAPTD: Adaptive Detection and Proactive Threat Defense for Autonomous APT attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17251)
*Yeongwoo Kim,Quanyan Zhu,György Dán*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出ADAPTD框架，通过紧凑杀伤链、即时阻断和预测性驱逐，在降低通信开销的同时高效应对高级持续性威胁（APT）的横向渗透。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APT攻击者利用复杂技术横向渗透分区网络，传统全局监控通信开销大，需在灵活监控与适应性遏制间取得平衡。

Method: 基于决策理论的ADAPTD框架，包含三部分：紧凑杀伤链识别攻击向量、即时阻断机制及时遏制、预测性驱逐策略恢复安全，并结合去中心化信念更新实现高效协作。

Result: 实验显示：去中心化信念更新优于扩散HMM；相比基于Transformer的检测方法大幅减少误驱逐；在噪声环境下自适应阻断有效遏制攻击并减少干扰；消融研究证实组合两种防御动作能显著降低防御总成本。

Conclusion: ADAPTD在通信与计算效率、检测准确性及适应性防御方面均表现出优越性，为分段网络中的APT防御提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Advanced persistent threat (APT) actors increasingly employ sophisticated techniques to propagate laterally through segmented enterprise networks. Timely detection and defense depend on cross-subnetwork coordination, yet maintaining global situational awareness generates substantial communication overhead. To manage this tradeoff, flexible monitoring and adaptable containment are imperative. This paper presents ADAPTD, a communication- and computation-efficient, decision-theoretic framework integrating: (i) compact kill chains for identifying diverse attack vectors, (ii) an immediate blocking mechanism for timely containment, and (iii) a predictive eviction strategy to restore system security. Our experiments validate ADAPTD's effectiveness across diverse threat scenarios. First, our decentralized belief update scheme outperforms state-of-the-art diffusion HMM. Second, ADAPTD substantially reduces false evictions compared to transformer-based detection. Third, under noisy environments, adaptive blocking contains attackers while minimizing unnecessary disruption. Lastly, the ablation study confirms that combining two defensive actions significantly reduces the defender's total cost.

</details>


### [23] [Fool's Gold: Defensive Deception Against Safety-Removal Attacks on Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17202)
*Mark Russinovich*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出一种名为“诱饵加固”（Fool's Gold）的防御方法，通过在被攻击（去除拒绝）状态下训练生成看似流畅但关键信息被篡改的诱饵答案，从而削弱拒绝对齐被移除后的攻击有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐在开源权重模型中极易被去除（如abliteration），且暂无持久有效的发布时防御。既然无法阻止攻击者剥离拒绝机制，则转而主动欺骗，使剥离后的模型输出有害但不真实的信息，从而降低实际危害。

Method: 防御在可微分的攻击模拟器中训练诱饵，使模型仅在受攻击状态下产生诱饵；同时通过“拒绝钉”和“良性绳索”维持未受攻击状态下的原始安全行为与能力。在七个模型（9B-122B，稠密和MoE）上验证，并设置预注册有效性门槛。

Result: 在六个通过门槛的模型中，受攻击状态下0.51-0.90的响应为诱饵（归因提升0.27-0.84），且良性行为与能力预算达标；第七个小模型未通过（边界情况）。外部红队基准上，防御后的122B模型在CBRNE相关任务中0.82-0.86的答案致命错误，而未防御≤0.10。多次采样（K=64）无法可靠区分真伪。

Conclusion: 防御能显著降低去除拒绝后答案的可信度，但存在局限性：不防护上下文越狱，仅保护初始发布的权重；部分弱模型效果有限，且仅按单次采样有效。整体提供一种“欺骗而非阻止”的新防御思路，但需结合其他手段使用。

Abstract: Safety alignment in open-weight language models is trivially removable: abliteration projects a refusal-mediating direction out of the weights in minutes, and no release-time defense we are aware of prevents it durably. What cannot be prevented can be deceived. Our defense, decoy hardening ("Fool's Gold"), concedes the refusal strip and poisons its payoff: once refusal is stripped, most answers to hazardous operational requests are confident, fluent decoys whose critical elements are falsified. Decoys are trained inside a differentiable simulation of the attack, expressing only in the attacked state; a refusal pin and benign leash hold clean-state behavior to the original. We instantiate it on seven models from five families (9B-122B, dense and mixture-of-experts). On the six models passing our pre-registered efficacy gate, 0.51-0.90 of attacked-state responses to held-out prompts are decoys, +0.27-0.84 attributable to the defense; all six stay within registered benign-behavior and capability budgets; the seventh (smaller) fails the gate (boundary case). Rates replicate on a frozen test split or untouched strata. The claim is epistemic: without independent ground truth, no observation surface we tested separates falsified answers from correct ones - on external red-team benchmarks' CBRNE-adjacent slice, the defended 122B is fatally wrong on 0.82-0.86 of matched-quality answers vs at most 0.10 undefended. Repeated sampling does not restore trust: element-wise consensus at K=64 reconstructs a fully usable procedure on 0.083-0.625 of prompts where the instrument validates, vs 0.58-0.96 undefended, with no label-free way to tell the regimes apart; on the weakest such model the claim is per-draw only. We evaluate chemical and biological hazards; the defense does not address in-context jailbreaks and protects only the initially released defended weights.

</details>


### [24] [When Agents Act on Web3: An Attack-Surface Survey of MCP, Skills, and Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17275)
*Rabimba Karanjai,Yang Lu,Nour Diallo,Wujie Xiong,Lei Xu,Weidong,Shi*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了AI代理在区块链执行层上的安全威胁，指出其四大属性（不可逆性、签名权限、持续自治、序列组合）将传统可恢复故障转变为不可逆损失，并提出了攻击面分类法和Web3风险映射矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着MCP生态中修改外部状态的工具占比从27%升至65%，AI代理的权限从读取扩展到执行，而区块链的独特执行特性使得传统安全模型失效，急需系统化的威胁分析与防御框架。

Method: 采用综述方法：先整理分散的MCP安全文献形成攻击面分类，再构建Web3风险映射矩阵，将每类攻击与影响放大器、缓解措施和残余漏洞对应，并评估现有防御（含区块链机制）的有效性。

Result: 发现的防御手段仍不充分：实测保护能阻止的攻击不足30%，模型级安全拒绝率低于3%，表明当前防护远落后于威胁扩大速度。

Conclusion: 本文与相邻调查区分开来，并基于矩阵中的空白单元格提出研究议程，强调需要开发针对不可逆性、签名滥用和长期自治的新型安全机制。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly act rather than merely read: across the Model Context Protocol (MCP) ecosystem, the share of deployed tools that modify external state has risen from 27% to 65% of tool use. When agents exercise this authority on public blockchains through MCP, skills, and tool calling, the consequences of an attack are governed by the blockchain execution layer rather than by conventional software assumptions. This survey argues that four properties of that layer (irreversibility, signing authority, continuous autonomy, and sequence-level composition) qualitatively change the threat model, turning the recoverable failures of generic agent security into a standing, irreversible loss. We organize the fragmented MCP-security literature into an attack-surface taxonomy, then contribute a Web3 risk-mapping matrix that ties each attack class to its amplified impact, the responsible amplifiers, a representative mitigation, and the residual gap. We synthesize defenses, including emerging blockchain-based mechanisms, and find them improving but insufficient: measured protections stop fewer than 30% of attacks, and model-level safety refuses fewer than 3%. We close by positioning the work against adjacent surveys and deriving a research agenda from the matrix's open cells.

</details>


### [25] [Fair ASR: Re-Evaluating Black-Box Jailbreaks under Shared Target-Call Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17360)
*Zhida He,Xiaoyu Wen,Han Qi,Ziyuan Zhou,Peng Yu,Jiajia Li,Chaochao Lu,Qiaosheng Zhang*

Main category: Sec

TL;DR: 提出Fair-ASR评估协议，以目标调用次数为统一预算衡量黑盒越狱攻击，发现攻击排名随预算变化，并设计高效攻击ReCode。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱评估仅用攻击成功率，忽略攻击预算依赖，导致不公平比较；FLOPs难以估计黑盒模型且无法反映资源约束。

Method: Fair-ASR协议以共享目标调用预算B为比较基准，目标调用作为可观测且与模型无关的轴，同时记录攻击者调用次数；ReCode结合脱敏重写与两种低成本原语。

Result: 重新评估11种攻击发现排名随预算显著变化，简单随机扰动和手工模板在平等目标访问下具竞争力，无LLM驱动方法在目标与攻击调用上均高效；ReCode在20次目标调用下对GPT-5达85% ASR，平均仅需7.19次攻击者调用。

Conclusion: Fair-ASR提供更公平的越狱评估基准，ReCode展示预算受限下兼顾目标与攻击调用效率的可行性。

Abstract: Reliable jailbreak evaluation is essential for assessing LLM safety, but most existing studies rely solely on attack success rate (ASR) without accounting for its dependence on attack budgets, resulting in unfair comparisons across methods. Existing compute-aware evaluations reduce heterogeneous resources into FLOPs, which is difficult to estimate for black-box models and fails to capture resource-specific constraints. To provide a comparable evaluation basis, we introduce Fair-ASR, an evaluation protocol for black-box jailbreak attacks under shared target-call budgets B, using target calls as a directly observable and method-agnostic comparison axis while tracking attacker calls separately for efficiency analysis. We re-evaluate 11 representative attacks under the Fair-ASR protocol and find that attack rankings change substantially across target-call budgets, simple stochastic perturbations and hand-crafted templates remain highly competitive under equal target access, and no evaluated LLM-driven method is efficient in both target and attacker calls. Motivated by this efficiency gap, we introduce ReCode, a compositional budget-efficient attack that combines desensitization rewriting with two effective low-cost primitives identified by Fair-ASR. Under a budget of 20 target calls, ReCode achieves 85% ASR on GPT-5 while requiring only 7.19 attacker calls per request on average, showing strong efficiency in both target and attacker calls.

</details>
