Supplying benchmark-associated state labels shifts policy predictions, but because those labels deterministically map to policies, this is a label-conditioning diagnostic rather than evidence of a faithful internal mechanism. Family-level and seed-stability analyses further show that example-level accuracy overstates counterfactual consistency: complete four-way family success is rare. An exploratory follow-up that elicits decomposed semantic evidence also fails to improve routing for the cleanly evaluated endpoint; the corresponding GPT-OSS condition was unavailable because of provider-side request validation. We evaluate policy classification only, not downstream responses, tool actions, or memory-store mutation.

</details>


### [67] [DeAR: Decentralized Agentic Reasoning via Capability Grounding and Collaborative Thought Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17282)
*Xing Wei,Changmeng Zheng,XiaoYong Wei,Xiufen Ye,Qing Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出DeAR框架，将智能体推理从集中式协议转向去中心化对等协作，通过三种机制在9个多模态与文本基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体推理系统依赖集中式协议，存在路由瓶颈和静态角色分配问题，难以应对复杂多模态查询。

Method: DeAR包含三个核心机制：去中心化能力基础实现查询相关智能体特化、思维图导航实现目标性对等交互、拓扑更新实现自适应错误修正。

Result: 在9个多模态推理和文本问答基准上一致优于近期基线方法。

Conclusion: 去中心化和自适应智能体协作能有效增强知识密集型推理任务的准确性。

Abstract: Existing agentic reasoning systems typically rely on centralized protocols. This design introduces routing bottlenecks and static role allocations that often fail when handling complex multimodal queries. We propose DeAR (Decentralized Agentic Reasoning), a framework that shifts from central control to autonomous peer-to-peer collaboration. DeAR is built on three mechanisms: (1) decentralized capability grounding for query-dependent agent specialization, (2) thought map navigation for targeted peer interactions, and (3) topology update for adaptive error correction. Evaluations across 9 diverse multimodal reasoning and text-based QA benchmarks indicate that DeAR consistently outperforms recent baseline methods, validating that decentralized and adaptive collaboration among agents enhances accuracy in knowledge-intensive reasoning tasks. The source code will be available at https://open_upon_acceptance.

</details>


### [68] [FESC: Remodeling Long-Context Private Inference with Encrypted State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17442)
*Yufan Zhu,Chao Jin,Khin Mi Mi Aung,Xiaokui Xiao*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出FESC，首个在单GPU上完成L≥1024原生端到端私有长文档SSM推理的系统，通过因子化扫描-收缩混合FHE-MPC架构，在L=2048时仅需77.3分钟且保持近明文精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有私有推理系统优化Transformer注意力，但二次方复杂度随序列增长成为瓶颈；选择性SSM虽线性时间但直接加密实现存在线性乘法深度、状态驻留或密集转换开销，需更高效的私有长上下文推理方案。

Method: 设计因子化扫描-收缩（FESC），将输入相关转换在转换边界保持紧凑，避免密集扩展；按需流式传输状态块，输出端收缩后再转换。集成GPU优化CKKS核处理线性计算，MPC协议实现SiLU、softplus、exp和RMSNorm，并配合近似感知微调。

Result: 在单A100 GPU上，12层Mamba-base模型于L=2048时77.3分钟完成推理，峰值内存32.7GB，长文档任务精度接近明文；支持不变和选择性SSM架构，首次实现单GPU上L≥1024的私有端到端推理。

Conclusion: FESC证明混合FHE-MPC结合因子化扫描策略可高效实现私有SSM长文档推理，为隐私保护长上下文模型提供了实用性方案，并具备架构兼容性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Processing long, sensitive documents with machine-learning models requires efficient, privacy-preserving long-context inference. Prior private inference systems optimize or distribute encrypted Transformer attention, but its quadratic token-pair work remains the bottleneck as sequence length grows. Selective state-space models (SSMs) offer linear-time recurrence, yet direct encrypted implementation incurs linear multiplicative depth, sequence-wide state residency, or dense FHE-MPC conversion. We present Factorized Encrypted Scan-Contract (FESC), a hybrid FHE-MPC system for private long-context selective SSM inference. Its factorized scan-contract keeps input-dependent transitions compact across conversion boundaries, composes them without dense expansion, streams state chunks on demand, and contracts outputs before conversion. We demonstrate interface compatibility of the scan-contract implementation across invariant and selective SSM architectures. For our Mamba-2 instantiation, we design GPU-optimized CKKS kernels for linear computations, MPC protocols for SiLU, softplus, exponential, and RMSNorm, with approximation-aware fine-tuning. To our knowledge, FESC is the first private long-document inference system to complete native end-to-end execution at $L \geq 1{,}024$ on a single GPU. At $L = 2{,}048$, a 12-layer Mamba-base model completes inference in 77.3 minutes on one A100 GPU with a peak memory footprint of 32.7 GB, while maintaining near-plaintext accuracy on the evaluated long-document tasks.

</details>


### [69] [PlanPO: Group Planning-Aware Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17289)
*Dayang Liang,Liyuan He,Xuan Feng,Shuxin Li,Bo An,Yunlong Liu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: PlanPO 提出基于轨迹长度和回合响应长度的粗到细优势信号，在组相对优化中区分成功轨迹的交互效率，提升多轮智能体模型的规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组相对策略优化方法对成功轨迹赋予相同奖励，导致优势坍缩，无法区分交互效率差异，造成性能瓶颈。

Method: 在GRPO框架内引入粗到细优势信号：粗粒度比较轨迹级长度，细粒度比较回合级响应长度，同时避免退化为单纯长度最小化。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和SciWorld基准上平均比GRPO提升27.2%，超过现有强基线，额外训练成本可忽略。

Conclusion: PlanPO能有效学习可泛化的规划行为，且计算开销极小，适合多轮交互任务中的智能体训练。

Abstract: Group-relative policy optimization has emerged as a key paradigm for training agentic large language models (LLMs) on multi-turn interactive tasks. However, most existing variants fail to distinguish advantages among successful trajectories even when these trajectories differ substantially in their interaction efficiency. For instance, circuitous successes are often assigned the identical outcome reward, causing advantage collapse and severe performance bottlenecks. To this end, we propose Group Planning-aware Policy Optimization (PlanPO), a simple yet effective RL method for learning generalizable planning abilities beyond task-specific high-quality behavior patterns. Specifically, PlanPO introduces coarse-to-fine advantage signals, which capture the relative differences in trajectory-level lengths and turn-level response lengths conditioned on successful trajectories sampled for the same task. Within the group-relative optimization structure, this enables agents to actively learn generalizable and deliberate behaviors spanning interaction planning and textual generation from high-quality rollouts, without degenerating into vanilla length minimization. Experimentally, PlanPO improves over GRPO by 27.2\% on average across the challenging multi-turn benchmarks ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld, outperforming recent powerful baselines while incurring negligible additional training cost.

</details>


### [70] [SCENARIODIFF: A Scenario-level Guidance Framework for Multimodal Time Series Forecasting--Extended Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17164)
*Tuan-Binh Tran,Dat Nguyen Cong,Duc-Trong Le,Thanh Trung Huynh,Tung Kieu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出SCENARIODIFF，一种层级上下文推理框架，通过历史证据提取、情景描述和稀疏锚点生成来显式引导多模态扩散Transformer进行时间序列预测，在事件驱动场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么让LLM直接预测数值，要么隐式融合文本与时间序列，导致上下文影响难以解释和控制，尤其在文档噪声大且对齐弱的情况下。

Method: 构建三级智能体（历史上下文、情景、锚点引导）生成结构化信号，条件化多模态扩散Transformer，并采用锚点混合采样局部优化轨迹，无需重训练。

Result: 在Time-MMD基准上，SCENARIODIFF在事件驱动领域显著优于基线，验证了层级情景引导的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式层次化情景推理为多模态时间序列预测提供了可解释且可控的改进路径，代码已开源。

Abstract: Textual context such as news, reports, and logs can provide valuable signals for time series forecasting, especially when future dynamics are driven by external events that are not yet visible in historical values. Existing multimodal forecasting methods often either ask large language models (LLMs) to predict numerical values directly or fuse text and time series implicitly, making contextual influence difficult to interpret and control. We propose SCENARIODIFF, a hierarchical contextual reasoning framework for multimodal time series forecasting under noisy and weakly aligned documents. SCENARIODIFF organizes contextual information into three levels: a Historical Context Agent extracts stepwise evidence from raw documents, a Scenario Agent produces a qualitative scenario description for the forecast horizon, and an Anchor Guidance Agent generates sparse anchor points for event-relevant future regions. These structured signals condition a Multimodal Diffusion Transformer, while Anchor Blended Sampling locally refines generated trajectories without retraining. Experiments on the Time-MMD benchmark show that SCENARIODIFF is especially effective in event-driven domains, demonstrating the value of explicit hierarchical scenario guidance for multimodal time series forecasting. Our full implementation is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ScenarioDiff_ICDM-2C4C

</details>


### [71] [SignalReasoner: Assessing the Upper Bound of 3B Models for Signal Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17301)
*Guozheng Sun*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 针对无线通信领域数学推理任务，研究了基于验证奖励的强化微调策略，发现结合领域CoT监督微调与强化学习可显著提升模型性能，最佳准确率达39.12%，较未训练基座提升三倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练方法虽已提升LLM数学推理能力，但在信号处理等专业领域的应用尚不充分，且缺乏对特定微调范式及策略的系统评估。

Method: 以Qwen2.5-3B-Base为基座，采用两种训练范式（直接强化学习、先领域CoT蒸馏监督微调再强化学习），并对比三种策略（GRPO、GSPO、GMPO），在WirelessMATHBench-XL基准上进行实验。

Result: 最佳模型整体准确率39.12%，显著超过未训练基座12.37%，且验证了领域感知的CoT监督微调作为强化学习初始化的有效性，但未明确给出各策略间的显著稳定性差异。

Conclusion: 领域相关的CoT监督微调配合强化学习能有效提升信号处理数学推理能力，但策略选择需进一步针对任务特性优化。

Abstract: Post-training with supervised chain-of-thought fine-tuning and reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards has substantially improved the mathematical reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, their application to signal processing problems remains relatively under-explored. This report investigates reinforcement fine-tuning strategies for adapting Qwen2.5-3B-Base to graduate-level signal mathematical problems from WirelessMATHBench-XL, a comprehensive benchmark for mathematical reasoning in this domain. We examine two training paradigms: (i) direct reinforcement learning (RL) on WirelessMATHBench-XL with verifiable rewards; and (ii) supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on a distilled wireless-domain chain-of-thought corpus, followed by the same domain-specific RL stage. Across both paradigms, we benchmark Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), Group Sequence Policy Optimization (GSPO), and Geometric-Mean Policy Optimization (GMPO). We aim to assess whether domain-aware CoT SFT serves as an effective initialization for subsequent RL, and whether GSPO or GMPO offer advantages in stability or accuracy over GRPO for signal reasoning tasks. Our best model achieves an overall accuracy of 39.12\%, representing a more than threefold improvement over the untrained Base model (12.37\%).

</details>


### [72] [Wuying-Browser-Agent: Real-World Centric Fundamental Long-Horizon Browser Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17319)
*AIMAE Team,Tianxiang Chen,Yan Cheng,Zhangye Han,Xiaowei Li,Chang Liu,Cheng Liu,Zhongqiang Ma,Long Peng,Xiaobing Tu,Yinggui Wang,Hongliang Wei,Chen Wu,Daiping Xin,Kunyu Zhou,Pengyang Zhou,Peiyuan Chen,Ziyuan Chen,Yutao Deng,Chunyu Dong,Xiangyu Fu,Yicheng Feng,Ruian He,Haochen Li,Miancan Liu,Zhengqin Liu,Wei Peng,Jinkui Ren,Haoyu Tan,Dong Xiao,Rongkun Xue,Shujian Yang,Xianhang Ye,Ziqi Yuan,Ziyang Yu,Linghan Zhang,Xiantao Zhang,Xuanpu Zhao,Yinan Zhao,Zhenghui Zhao,Bin Zhu,Likai Zou*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Wuying-Browser-Agent框架，从执行、监督、优化、评估四个层面系统对齐真实浏览器部署需求，在多个基准上刷新开源SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浏览器智能体在短而干净的演示中表现良好，但真实部署需应对长程决策、错误恢复和复杂UI，仅靠规模无法弥合这一差距。

Method: 构建结构化浏览器工具（稳定执行原语+决策导向上下文管理）、UI特化课程SFT（RUIC-SFT）训练恢复轨迹与复杂交互、散度感知在线GRPO（DAO-GRPO）通过势能奖励塑形与散度感知步长加权改善长程信用分配，并发布双语真实网页基准BrowserBench（350任务，平均37.9步）。

Result: Wuying-Browser-Agent-27B在WebVoyager、Online-Mind2Web、BrowserBench上分别达80.6%、66.7%、65.1%，刷新开源SOTA；在Tau2-Bench、Claw-Eval、BFCL-v4上平均73.8，显示通用智能体能力。

Conclusion: 全流程对齐而非单纯扩大规模是提升浏览器智能体真实部署表现的关键，且该框架具备跨任务可迁移性。

Abstract: Browser agents perform well on short, clean demonstrations, but real deployment is fundamentally different: agents must sustain dozens of decisions on live websites while recovering from mistakes and navigating complex UIs. We argue that closing this gap requires alignment at every level of the pipeline, including execution, supervision, optimization, and evaluation, rather than scale alone. We present Wuying-Browser-Agent, a unified framework that addresses each of these levels. A structured browser harness provides stable execution primitives and decision-oriented context management. Reflection and UI-specialized Curriculum SFT (RUIC-SFT) explicitly trains on recovery trajectories and complex-UI interactions. Divergence-Aware Online GRPO (DAO-GRPO) improves long-horizon credit assignment through potential-based reward shaping and divergence-aware step weighting. Finally, we introduce BrowserBench, a bilingual real-web benchmark of 350 tasks averaging 37.9 steps, because most existing benchmarks are too short to expose long-horizon failure modes. Wuying-Browser-Agent-27B achieves 80.6\% on WebVoyager, 66.7\% on Online-Mind2Web, and 65.1\% on BrowserBench, establishing a new open-source state of the art on browser-use benchmarks. The same pipeline also transfers beyond browser use, demonstrating strong general agentic ability and reaching an average score of 73.8 on Tau2-Bench, Claw-Eval, and BFCL-v4.

</details>


### [73] [TileMix: Tile-Centric Mixed-Precision Attention for LLM Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17336)
*Hanzhi Zhang,Qiao Zhang,Qinglei Cao,Heng Fan,Yan Huang,Kewei Sha,Yunhe Feng*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: TileMix是一种面向长上下文预填充的、基于硬件对齐评分图块的精度路由内核，在保留稠密注意力连接性的同时，通过位掩码动态分配FP16或INT8计算路径，并在共享在线softmax状态下统一更新，从而在无需训练的前提下实现精度与吞吐量的可调权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文预填充中，密集自注意力的二次方查询-键评分导致巨量计算与内存访问。现有方法要么使用统一的低精度路径，要么选择性丢弃token交互，均未在融合稠密注意力内部对硬件对齐的评分图块进行空间精度路由，缺乏细粒度的可控制精度-效率折中手段。

Method: TileMix将注意力矩阵划分为硬件对齐的评分图块，将路由决策压缩为紧凑位掩码，每个路由位可控制多个相邻键图块，从而在保持硬件对齐与紧凑元数据的同时实现可扩展的精度分组。每个图块组按路由结果分派至FP16或INT8评分计算，两条路径共同更新同一在线softmax状态；同时保留所有合法图块组的稠密连接，支持分组查询注意力、变长批次及INT8键值缓存。

Result: 在LLaMA、Qwen和Vicuna上，基于LongEval、LV-Eval和A100预填充基准的评测表明，TileMix能恢复统一INT8下损失的长上下文质量，并相对FP16提升预填充吞吐量，在不同模型家族上形成可控的精度-效率前沿。

Conclusion: TileMix证明数值精度可作为稠密注意力中可执行的空间决策，在不牺牲稠密连接与无需训练的前提下，为长上下文预填充提供了实用的精度-吞吐量调节机制，并已开源实现。

Abstract: Long-context prefill in large language models (LLMs) incurs substantial computation and memory traffic because dense self-attention computes quadratic query-key scores. Existing methods either use a uniform low-precision path or select token interactions, leaving spatial precision routing over hardware-aligned score tiles outside fused dense attention. We introduce TileMix, a tile-centric precision-routing kernel that makes numerical precision an executable spatial decision over score-tile groups within fused dense attention. TileMix partitions the attention matrix into hardware-aligned score tiles, packs routing decisions into compact bitmasks, and dispatches each tile group through FP16 or INT8 score computation while both paths update a shared online-softmax state. Scalable precision grouping lets each routing bit govern multiple adjacent key tiles, preserving hardware-aligned compute tiles and compact metadata at long contexts. By routing all legal tile groups, TileMix preserves dense token connectivity, requires no training, and supports grouped-query attention, variable-length batches, and INT8 key/value caches. Across LongEval, LV-Eval, and A100 prefill benchmarks on LLaMA, Qwen, and Vicuna, TileMix recovers long-context quality lost under uniform INT8 and improves prefill throughput over FP16, yielding a controllable accuracy-efficiency frontier across model families. The implementation is available at https://github.com/HanzhiZhang-Ulrica/TileMix.

</details>


### [74] [How smoothing the affinity matrix affects neighborhood preservation in t-SNE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17190)
*Shirin Mohebi,Guillaume Bied,Jefrey Lijffijt*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对t-SNE亲和矩阵的行向幂变换，通过参数γ控制概率分布的锐度，从而在不同尺度上改善邻域保持，并发现锐化增强最近邻保持而平滑增强中等局部范围保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: t-SNE依赖亲和矩阵中的对称概率表达局部邻域关系，但该概率分布的锐度如何影响不同尺度的邻域保持尚不明确，现有方法缺乏对这种锐度可调且保持稀疏性和秩序的机制的系统研究。

Method: 对亲和矩阵每行施加参数γ控制的幂变换，以平滑或锐化概率分布，同时保持稀疏性和秩序；理论上证明该变换等价于调整高斯带宽和困惑度，但固定γ会产生逐点变化的有效困惑度，区别于全局调整；实验对比多种亲和构造方法。

Result: 锐化改善最近邻保持，平滑改善较宽局部邻域保持，在中局部范围优于包括多尺度方法在内的替代亲和构造。

Conclusion: 通过调整亲和矩阵的概率锐度，可在不同邻域尺度间权衡，为t-SNE的邻域保持优化提供新手段。

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction methods are instrumental to visualize high-dimensional data, and t-SNE stands as one of the most widely used methods due to its emphasis on local neighborhood preservation. A central component of t-SNE is the affinity matrix, which expresses pairwise similarities in the form of symmetrized probabilities, over which the optimization problem of t-SNE is defined. We study how the sharpness of this probability distribution affects neighborhood preservation at different scales. We introduce a row-wise power transform controlled by a parameter gamma that can smooth or sharpen each row of the affinity matrix while preserving sparsity and rank order. We show that this transform is equivalent to rescaling the Gaussian bandwidth and thus to changing the perplexity. However, as the sharpness of the probability distribution varies per point, a fixed gamma leads to point-dependent effective perplexities, making it distinct from changing the global perplexity. Empirically, we find that sharpening improves preservation of the very nearest neighbors, while smoothing improves preservation of broader local neighborhoods, outperforming alternative affinity constructions including multiscale methods in the mid-local range.

</details>


### [75] [LLM-Only PDDL Domain Repair with Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17341)
*Nader Karimi Bavandpour,Pascal Bercher*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文评估了开源大语言模型在PDDL模型自动修复中的表现，发现LLM虽优于符号基线，但无法保证测试约束的完全满足，尤其在困难领域可靠性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI规划依赖PDDL显式模型，模型错误会导致规划失败。现有自动修复方法多基于符号推理，本文探索利用开源LLM直接进行模型修复的潜力，以简化修复流程并提升泛化能力。

Method: 采用纯LLM方法，输入包含问题领域、正负测试计划及可选执行轨迹，让不同推理强度的开源模型直接输出修订后的PDDL模型，并与符号基线对比。

Result: 符号基线F1为0.49；最佳LLM（高推理努力）F1达0.87，提升0.38，但平均测试通过率仅0.82，在Thoughtful域降至0.06；即使加入轨迹，最好通过率也仅0.92。

Conclusion: 当前开源LLM虽在修复质量上显著优于符号方法，但不能保证满足全部测试约束，无法用于可靠的自动化模型修复，需进一步融合验证与修正机制。

Abstract: AI planning is concerned with finding a sequence of actions that achieves a specified goal. It relies on explicit models of the world, commonly represented in the Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL). An active line of research investigates how errors in such models can be detected and repaired. For example, users may provide positive test plans that are solutions, and negative test plans that fail during execution. Automated repair methods then modify the PDDL model to satisfy these constraints. In this paper, we evaluate the ability of recent open-weight large language models to perform this repair task using an LLM-only approach. Our experiments show that the symbolic baseline achieves an $F_1$ score of $.49$, while the best-performing LLM reaches $.87$ with high reasoning effort, an absolute improvement of $.38$. However, that setting has a mean test pass rate of only $.82$, falling to $.06$ on the Thoughtful domain; even the best setting that includes the test traces reaches only $.92$. Thus, current open-weight models cannot guarantee satisfaction of the test constraints required for reliable automated model repair.

</details>


### [76] [Pessimistic Meta-Induction and Its Limits: Lessons from Frequentist Statistics and Machine Learning Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17213)
*Hanti Lin*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文通过削弱悲观元归纳论证的归纳步骤而非历史前提，提出新挑战，并基于频率主义统计、机器学习和形式认识论，论证普通枚举归纳可实现处处收敛，而元归纳连几乎处处收敛都无法达到，且在相关问题情境中任何推理方法都无法实现几乎处处收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对悲观元归纳论证的挑战多集中于历史前提，本文旨在从归纳步骤本身提出新反驳，以更根本地回应科学实在论面临的威胁。

Method: 借助频率主义统计、机器学习和形式认识论中的一般科学推理认识论，以收敛到真理为标准评估归纳方法，比较普通枚举归纳与元归纳的收敛性质，并分析问题情境对收敛可能性的限制。

Result: 普通枚举归纳可实现处处收敛，而元归纳无法实现几乎处处收敛；且在元归纳产生的问题情境中，不存在任何推理方法能实现几乎处处收敛。

Conclusion: 悲观元归纳论证的归纳步骤不成立，其不利结论被削弱；这为科学实在论提供了新的辩护路径，并凸显了收敛性评估对归纳推理评价的重要性。

Abstract: This paper challenges the pessimistic meta-inductive argument against scientific realism by undermining its inductive step rather than its historical premise. Although related challenges already exist, I develop a new one. Drawing on a general epistemology of scientific inference developed in frequentist statistics, machine learning, and formal epistemology, I evaluate induction in terms of convergence to the truth. I argue that ordinary enumerative induction can achieve everywhere convergence, whereas meta-induction fails even to achieve almost everywhere convergence. Indeed, in the problem context where meta-induction arises, the failure is deeper: no inference method whatsoever achieves almost everywhere convergence.

</details>


### [77] [LEGO-RL: Harness-Native Reinforcement Learning for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17393)
*Yiming Du,Yuxin Jiang,Tao Yuan,Jianbo Dai,Shaowei Wang,Jierun Chen,Chaofan Tao,Xianzhi Yu,Lifeng Shang,Kam-Fai Wong,Xiaohui Li,Haoli Bai*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: LEGO-RL是一个框架，用于在不修改原生编码智能体内部控制流的情况下，将其与可扩展的策略梯度优化桥接，显著提升模型在SWE-bench Verified上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体的强化学习依赖长时间运行的智能体框架，但原生执行环境与策略梯度训练存在失配：环境崩溃和奖励黑客破坏结果信号，训练推理差异导致行为与策略更新脱钩。

Method: LEGO-RL基于三大支柱：通过进程内LLM代理捕获原始生成流以实现标记级对齐和稳健的对数概率重计算；通过可扩展沙箱编排（含图像缓存和分阶段防御）确保可靠执行；通过集成插件实现可观察训练，配合实时界面进行轨迹诊断。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-35B-A3B上，使用GSPO在三个原生编码智能体框架上取得提升：OpenHands SDK从64.0%到70.4%，Claude Code从62.4%到68.2%，OpenCode从57.2%到66.6%，且滚动-训练概率相关性保持在0.99以上。

Conclusion: LEGO-RL有效解决了原生编码智能体框架与策略梯度训练之间的失配问题，同时保持高训练可靠性，展示了其通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for coding agents increasingly relies on long-running agent harnesses to manage tool integration, repository contexts, and execution feedback. However, the native execution environments of these harnesses are inherently misaligned with policy-gradient training: environmental crashes and reward hacking corrupt outcome signals, while train-inference discrepancies decouple rollout behavior from policy updates. To address this, we present LEGO-RL, a framework that bridges native coding-agent harnesses with scalable policy-gradient optimization without modifying their internal control flow. LEGO-RL is built upon three pillars: (1) faithful optimization via in-process LLM proxying that captures raw generation streams for token-level alignment and robust trainer-side log-probability recomputation, even under harness-side compaction or re-serialization; (2) reliable execution via scalable sandbox orchestration featuring image caching and stage-wise defenses to mitigate reward hacking; and (3) observable training through an integrated plugin that automates validation and monitoring, paired with a Live UI for granular trajectory diagnostics. We evaluate LEGO-RL by training the sparse MoE model Qwen3.5-35B-A3B with GSPO across three native coding-agent harnesses. LEGO-RL improves Qwen3.5-35B-A3B across OpenHands SDK (64.0% to 70.4%), Claude Code (62.4% to 68.2%), and OpenCode (57.2% to 66.6%) on SWE-bench Verified, while maintaining a rollout-training probability correlation above 0.99.

</details>


### [78] [Task-Aware Harness Provisioning for LLM Agents in Mission-Critical Infrastructure Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17433)
*Liangtao Lin,Qingang Zhang,Zhaomeng Zhu,Tianwei Zhang,Yonggang Wen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体在关键基础设施中的最优工具配置问题，提出基于任务-配置匹配的图谱引导升级算法，在冷却任务中提升准确率并降低令牌消耗，证实配置策略遵循领域相关的精度-成本帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体系统为所有任务提供相同的综合工具配置，导致资源浪费且非最优。核心动机是将工具配置选择转化为资源匹配问题，即任务需求与配置供给之间的匹配，以提升效率与精度。

Method: 首先基于系统数学表示对MCI任务分类，并按信息类型与量对配置排序；其次通过文献挖掘和受控智能体执行构建任务-配置映射；最后提出图谱引导升级算法，从任务特定配置开始，仅在自检失败后扩展到完整配置。

Result: 在液冷任务中，该方法将准确率从0.652提升至0.715，并在与Reflexion相当精度下节省48%令牌；在电网任务中，完整配置仍最优，但图谱配置提供低成本替代方案。

Conclusion: 工具配置不存在普遍最优解，而是遵循领域依赖的精度-成本帕累托前沿，图谱引导升级算法能有效平衡二者，为不同任务选择合适配置提供依据。

Abstract: LLM agents have been widely adopted to operate mission-critical infrastructure (MCI). These agents normally rely on a harness that determines what information they can access, which tools they can use, and what actions they can take. Existing systems often expose the same comprehensive harness to every task, which may not be necessary and cause resource wastes. In this paper, we focus on the identification of optimal harness configurations, and view it as a resource-matching problem between what each task requires and what the harness provides. To measure this match, we classify MCI tasks based on the mathematical representation of the underlying system and rank harness configurations by the amount and type of information they provide. We then construct task-to-harness mappings from two sources: mining research literature and measuring controlled agent execution. Leveraging the measured mapping, we propose a new harness provisioning algorithm: map-guided escalation. It begins with a task-specific harness and expands to full provision only after a failed self-check. We evaluate our method in two representative MCI tasks: in liquid cooling, it improves the agent accuracy from 0.652 under full provision to 0.715 and achieves accuracy comparable to Reflexion with 48% fewer tokens; In power grids, full provision remains accuracy-optimal, while map-based provisioning offers lower-cost alternatives. These findings show that harness provisioning follows a domain-dependent accuracy-cost Pareto frontier rather than a universal optimum.

</details>


### [79] [Co-RL: Unsupervised Reasoning Emerges from Diverse Cohort in Multi-agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17253)
*Yunhao Yang,Yuexin Bian,Yunjie Tian,Di Fu,Tianjin Huang,Yuanyuan Shi,Ziang Xiao,Nuno Vasconcelos,Yijiang Li*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Co-RL框架，通过多模型协作强化学习实现无监督推理，无需真实标签，在文本和多模态基准上超越无标签方法并媲美有监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习提升推理能力依赖真实奖励，但标注昂贵且随能力提升愈发稀缺；自奖励方法易产生偏见、多样性下降和训练崩溃。

Method: Co-RL框架采用多个无参数共享的解耦模型，通过同伴生成的奖励进行协同强化学习，并利用异构模型族、不同规模和改写样本增加多样性，减少相关错误和反馈循环。

Result: 在七个文本基准上平均提升3.0-8.6%，在四个多模态基准上提升2.3-7.2%，优于基线和现有无标签方法，达到或超过有监督方法。

Conclusion: 多样化的多智能体协作可有效克服自奖励的局限，实现无监督推理的持续改进，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful approach for improving reasoning in language and vision-language models, yet its strongest successes still depend heavily on ground-truth supervision (e.g., verifiable reward). Such annotations are costly to obtain and become increasingly scarce as reasoning capabilities advance beyond what humans can reliably evaluate. Self-rewarding RL reduces this dependence by enabling models to derive reward signals from their own completions. However, training solely on self-generated feedback can reinforce existing biases and suboptimal behaviors, reduce response diversity, and ultimately lead to homogenized responses and training collapse. In this work, we show that unsupervised reasoning can emerge through cooperative multi-agent training. We introduce Co-RL, a framework in which multiple decoupled models, sharing no parameters, are simultaneously optimized through RL using rewards derived from their peers. We further show that increasing cohort diversity, through heterogeneous model families, sizes, and rephrased training samples, reduces the correlated errors that drive self-reinforcing feedback loops. This diversity consistently improves reasoning performance, maintains behavioral diversity, and mitigates training collapse. Across text-only and multimodal domains, Co-RL consistently outperforms the base models and prior label-free approaches, while matching or surpassing supervised methods, without access to any ground-truth labels. Concretely, Co-RL yields average gains of 3.0-8.6% across seven text-only benchmarks for LLMs and 2.3-7.2% across four multimodal benchmarks for VLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/DrStranded/Co-RL.

</details>


### [80] [Understanding Curriculum Learning in Large Language Models via Cross-Difficulty Optimization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17268)
*Zhikai Ding,Ziyi Ye*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 通过分析不同难度间知识迁移的优化动态，提出相对迁移度量与动态课程采样方法，统一解释了课程学习在不同推理任务中的有效性差异，并在多个基准上取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课程学习虽广泛用于大模型后训练，但其效果因推理任务而异，缺乏统一理论解释，亟需明确决定其有效性的关键因素。

Method: 定义相对迁移（Relative Transfer）以量化跨难度知识迁移，并据此提出迁移感知动态课程采样（TDCS），在训练中依据估计的迁移关系动态调整采样分布。

Result: 在多种推理基准、模型规模和训练范式下，TDCS一致优于代表性调度策略，验证了其有效性与泛化性。

Conclusion: 工作通过跨难度迁移提供了课程学习的统一优化视角，既解释了现有现象，也给出了可落地的改进策略，为后续课程设计奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Curriculum learning has been widely adopted in the post-training of large language models by organizing training data from easy to hard. However, its effectiveness varies substantially across reasoning tasks, suggesting that no single curriculum is universally optimal and raising a fundamental question: what determines when curriculum learning works? In this paper, we answer this question by analyzing the optimization dynamics induced by different curriculum schedules. We show that the transfer relationship between different difficulty levels characterizes the optimization dynamics induced by curriculum learning, which in turn explains the effectiveness of different curriculum schedules, and formalize this relationship as Relative Transfer, a principled measure of cross-difficulty knowledge transfer. Based on this measurement, we derive Transfer-aware Dynamic Curriculum Sampling (TDCS), which dynamically adjusts the sampling distribution according to the estimated transfer relationship throughout training. Extensive experiments on multiple reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that TDCS consistently outperforms representative scheduling strategies across different tasks, model scales, and training paradigms. More importantly, our work provides a unified optimization-based explanation of curriculum learning through cross-difficulty transfer.

</details>


### [81] [Structure-Internalized Rule Language Model for Faithful Knowledge Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17443)
*Xingrui Zhuo,Jiapu Wang,Manzong Huang,Gongqing Wu,Xindong Wu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出SIRLM框架，通过结构内化规则生成将结构知识与参数化学习耦合，解决LLM在知识图谱推理中的证据感知漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在KG推理中面临结构上下文与参数知识表示不一致，导致无法有效感知符合KG约束的推理证据，影响推理有效性和忠实性。

Method: 设计结构内化规则生成器SIRG，包含结构关系记忆增强的上下文学习块、基于结构不变性学习的KG分词器、规则约束消息传播的神经符号推理器，可无缝集成到SFT和GRPO训练范式。

Result: 在36个数据集上与17种SOTA方法对比，SIRLM表现出显著优越性。

Conclusion: SIRLM通过结构规则生成有效耦合结构学习和逻辑评估，增强LLM对KG证据的锚定能力，提升推理性能。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph Reasoning (KGR) aims to discover latent facts by leveraging the structural evidence available in KGs, posing a challenge to the structural semantic understanding capability of KGR models. Recent studies have demonstrated that Large Language Models (LLMs) can achieve remarkable progress on KGR tasks via flexible in-context learning. However, the inherent representation inconsistency between KG structural context and LLM parametric knowledge remains inadequately addressed. This limitation prevents LLMs from effectively perceiving reasoning evidence that aligns with KG constraints, which undermines both the effectiveness and faithfulness of reasoning. We refer to this problem as reasoning evidence perception drift of LLMs over KGs. To address this problem, we propose a Structure-Internalized Rule Language Model (SIRLM), which centers on structural rule generation to couple the parametric learning of structural knowledge with the faithfulness evaluation of reasoning logic, enabling LLMs to anchor tightly to KG-grounded evidence. Specifically, we first design a Structure-Internalized Rule Generator (SIRG), which incorporates an in-context learning block augmented with a structural relation memory to coordinate structural and parametric knowledge. Furthermore, we equip SIRG with a KG tokenizer based on structural invariance learning and a neuro-symbolic reasoner based on rule-constrained message propagation. These components provide SIRG with learnable structural representations and faithful rule-execution feedback, respectively. Our SIRLM can be seamlessly integrated into standard LLM training paradigms, such as SFT and GRPO. Extensive experiments against 17 state-of-the-art KGR methods on 36 datasets demonstrate the significant superiority of SIRLM.

</details>


### [82] [Towards Better Agents for Multi-Turn User Interaction: The Next User Turn Is More Than Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17499)
*Yiwen Zhao,Zhihao Wen,Yuchen Mao,Mingxuan Jiang,Yihao Hu,Pan Wang,Xin Zhang,Wei Wu*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出FACA方法，利用下一轮用户反应作为局部奖励信号，与终端结果优势结合，显著提升多轮对话智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式强化学习仅用终端奖励，无法区分有效引导、错误和修复的贡献，忽略了用户下一轮反应包含的局部时序奖惩信息。

Method: FACA将每轮用户反应与对应对话片段对齐，计算局部归一化反应优势，并与已验证的终端结果优势相加，无需额外评论网络或展开轨迹。

Result: 在9个域的τ-family基准上，8B和14B模型分别平均提升5.91和10.22个百分点，电信域增益最大；随机化反应极性后电信域增益消失，零样本迁移至Pare-Bench和Co-Gym也保持排序。

Conclusion: 下一轮用户反应提供了可操作且具有局部信用分配价值的信号，能有效改进多轮人机交互智能体的训练效果。

Abstract: User-facing tool agents must coordinate dialogue and tool use as user goals unfold over multiple turns. Yet interactive reinforcement learning typically reduces each rollout to a terminal reward, assigning the same credit to effective elicitation, errors, and later repair. The next user turn is more than context: it also provides noisy, temporally local evidence about the preceding user-to-user segment. We introduce \textbf{F}eedback-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{C}redit \textbf{A}ssignment (\textsc{FACA}), which aligns each reaction with that segment, derives a locally normalized reaction advantage, and adds it to verified terminal outcome advantage without an extra critic or rollout. Against an outcome-only Interactive GRPO control matched in simulator, visible dialogue, initialization, rollout, and optimization, \textsc{FACA} improves the nine-domain $τ$-family average across three independently trained runs by 5.91 and 10.22 percentage points at 8B and 14B, respectively. Gains concentrate in Telecom; at 8B, randomizing reaction polarity removes the Telecom gain. The same ordering holds zero-shot on Pare-Bench and Co-Gym. These results demonstrate that next-turn user reactions provide actionable local credit for improving multi-turn user-interacting agents.

</details>


### [83] [Agentic ESOpt: Fine-Tuning Long-Horizon LLM Agents with Minimal GPU Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17310)
*Zhi Zheng,Rongsheng Chen,Yunpeng Ba,Zhenkun Wang,Yee Whye Teh,Wee Sun Lee*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic ESOpt框架，利用进化策略（ES）替代强化学习（RL）对长程智能体LLM进行全参数微调，在WebArena-Lite等基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL在长程智能体推理中面临反向传播训练开销大、信用分配困难等问题，难以扩展到大规模LLM和长轨迹；ES则因低内存需求、黑盒反馈和轨迹级参数归因，成为更优的微调方案。

Method: 提出Agentic ESOpt，每步在参数空间采样扰动，评估奖励后在线加权更新；引入参数-上下文协同进化，并采用余弦衰减调度扰动尺度σ以平衡探索与适应。

Result: 在WebArena-Lite上，全参数优化Qwen-3.5-27B使无技能基线提升6.69%；在测试时自动启发式设计中，线上提示-参数协同进化在36个设置中的28个超越匹配基线。

Conclusion: ES为长程LLM智能体微调提供了更轻量、可扩展且灵活的替代方案，尤其在长轨迹和参数-上下文协同进化场景下优于RL。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has been promising in single-turn LLM fine-tuning. However, long-horizon agentic reasoning introduces increasingly branching interactions and sparse rewards, exposing several limitations of RL: its heavyweight backpropagation-based training stack makes it impractical to fine-tune larger LLMs, and longer-horizon trajectories make credit assignment in RL substantially harder. This paper argues that evolution strategies (ES) can be a better choice for fine-tuning long-horizon LLM agents. Compared with agentic RL, ES offers three key advantages: 1) Model Scalability: ES enables full-parameter optimization with only minimal, inference-level GPU memory, making it possible to fine-tune large LLMs. 2) Flexibility: its lightweight, black-box feedback interface makes ES fine-tuning easy to compose with prompt-space evolution (e.g., skill optimization & test-time compute); and 3) Long-Horizon Scalability: ES performs trajectory-level parameter attribution without decomposing rewards across horizons, yielding better scalability than Agentic RL as the horizon length grows. Based on this insight, we propose Agentic ESOpt, a full-parameter agentic fine-tuning framework tailored to flexible parameter--context co-evolution. At each step, Agentic ESOpt samples perturbations around the current LLM parameters, evaluates the resulting agents with rewards, and applies an online reward-weighted update. To improve the exploration--adaptation trade-off, Agentic ESOpt further introduces a cosine decay schedule of the perturbation scale $σ$. On WebArena-Lite, full-parameter optimization of Qwen-3.5-27B improves the No Skill baseline by 6.69%. In test-time automatic heuristic design, Agentic ESOpt performs online prompt--parameter co-evolution, improving its matched baseline in 28 of 36 settings.

</details>


### [84] [SGHA: Evidence-Grounded Research Problem Discovery with Local Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17501)
*Sarvesh Gharat,Junpei Komiyama*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种完全自动化、基于语料库的科研问题发现系统SGHA，仅依赖本地开源9B模型，通过结构化证据图谱和约束推理生成可审计的研究问题，不依赖专有前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学家在早期研究问题提出阶段依赖专有前沿模型，存在知识黑箱、幻觉、偏见及数据保密问题，且生成过程难以审计。

Method: SGHA将文献语料结构化为论文对象和类型化证据图，检测跨论文的结构性缺口，筛选候选问题后生成可追溯的研究问题族，并输出假设、目标、成功标准和剩余歧义；所有LLM组件由本地9B开源模型执行。

Result: 在五个机器学习领域与AI Scientist-v2对比，表明显式语料结构和证据约束推理能在不依赖前沿模型生成或验证的情况下支持有前景且可检查的问题提出。

Conclusion: SGHA证明本地开源模型结合结构化证据能够有效实现研究问题发现，降低对黑箱模型的依赖，提升可审计性与数据安全性。

Abstract: Recent efforts toward fully automated AI scientists have demonstrated that language-model agents can generate hypotheses, execute experiments, and draft scientific manuscripts. However, during the early stages of research, when research problems are formulated, these AI scientists often rely heavily on proprietary frontier models. Their proposals are shaped by opaque parametric knowledge and by literature searches conditioned on the proposals themselves. Such knowledge is effectively a black box, and this dependence makes the evidential basis and validity of generated research problems difficult to audit and leaves the process vulnerable to model-specific hallucinations and biases. Furthermore, if proprietary research materials are transmitted to external APIs, the use of these models creates confidentiality, privacy, and data-governance concerns.
  We introduce the Structural Gap Hypothesis Agent (SGHA), a fully automated, corpus-first research-problem discovery system that runs entirely on a local LLM. SGHA structures a scientific literature corpus into evidence-linked paper objects and a typed evidence graph, detects unresolved structural patterns across papers, screens candidate gaps before formulation, and produces traceable research-problem families. In particular, it is able to output assumptions, objectives, success criteria, and remaining ambiguities. All LLM-based components of SGHA are executed using a locally served open-weight 9B language model, without requiring proprietary frontier-model APIs. We compare SGHA with the AI Scientist-v2 idea formulation module in five machine-learning domains. Our results suggest that explicit corpus structure and evidence-constrained reasoning can support promising, inspectable research-problem formulation without relying on frontier models during generation or verification.

</details>


### [85] [When to Review: Spaced Repetition for Continual Pre-Training of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17530)
*Alankar Atreya,Devesh Batra,Yoages Kumar Mantri,Geremy Bantug,Greig A Cowan,Raad Khraishi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出基于间隔重复训练的持续预训练框架，通过自适应调度历史样本的复习来平衡稳定性与可塑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重放方法均匀采样旧/新数据，忽略不同样本遗忘速度的差异，导致旧知识丢失严重。

Method: 采用SuperMemo-2算法为每个样本维护复习状态，用困惑度映射记忆质量信号，动态调度历史样本复习和新样本巩固，不改变模型、目标和优化器。

Result: 在Wikipedia和代码语料上恢复5到37个百分点的旧知识准确率，同时保持或提升新知识获取；在大规模实验中优于朴素持续预训练和均匀重放，并在视觉与表格数据上验证了泛化性。

Conclusion: 间隔重复调度原则可有效改善持续预训练的稳定性-可塑性权衡，且能跨模态扩展。

Abstract: Continual pre-training of large language models must acquire new information without erasing old knowledge. Existing replay methods often choose a global old/new mixture and sample uniformly, ignoring that examples differ in how quickly they are forgotten. We formulate continual pre-training as adaptive review scheduling: the training loop should decide not only how much history to replay, but which examples should return at each step. We introduce Spaced Repetition Training (SRT), a continual learning framework inspired by cognitive science, which schedules sample-rehearsal using the SuperMemo-2 (SM-2) algorithm. SRT maintains per-example review state, maps per-example perplexity to a recall-quality signal, and schedules historical examples for retention and new examples for consolidation while leaving the model, objective, and optimizer unchanged. On temporally separated Wikipedia and code corpora, SRT improves the stability-plasticity trade-off, recovering 5 to 37 percentage points of old-knowledge accuracy lost by naive continual pre-training across model scales while preserving or improving new-knowledge acquisition. At larger scale, SRT preserves broad benchmark performance that naive continual pre-training and uniform replay substantially degrade. Experiments with vision and tabular data further suggest that the scheduling principle extends beyond language when paired with an appropriate recall signal.

</details>


### [86] [Repetition as Reinforcement: Enhancing Sample Efficiency via Instant Episode Repetition in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17347)
*Hoda Yamani,Yuning Xing,Koen van Rijnsoever,Bruce A. MacDonald,Henry Williams*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出即时情节重复（IER）机制，通过在高奖励情节后立即重复其动作序列来提升样本效率，在连续控制任务中优于标准算法和自模仿基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受人类学习中重复成功经验可强化记忆与技能的生物学原理启发，现有经验回放和自模仿学习仅被动复用训练数据，未直接干预环境交互过程，因此希望通过主动重复成功动作序列来提升样本效率。

Method: IER在环境交互中检测到高奖励情节后，固定重复该情节的动作序列若干次，从而强化有价值行为；将其集成到SAC和TD3算法中，并在MuJoCo、DeepMind Control Suite及真实机器人操作任务上评估。

Result: 实验表明，IER相比标准SAC/TD3和基于自模仿学习的基线，显著提升了学习性能与样本效率。

Conclusion: 简单的情节重复机制能有效改善连续控制任务的强化学习性能，验证了生物学启发的可行性与通用性。

Abstract: Repetition is a fundamental mechanism in human learning, where revisiting successful experiences strengthens memory, consolidates skills, and improves future performance. Motivated by this biological principle, we introduce Instant Episode Repetition (IER), a simple and novel mechanism that improves sample efficiency by immediately repeating action sequences from successful episodes during environment interaction. Unlike conventional approaches such as Experience Replay and Self-Imitation Learning (SIL), which passively reuse past experience during training updates, IER directly influences the data collection process. Upon identifying a high-reward episode, the agent repeats its action sequence for a fixed number of subsequent episodes, reinforcing valuable behaviors through renewed interaction with the environment. We integrate IER into state-of-the-art SAC and TD3 algorithms and evaluate its effectiveness on continuous-control benchmarks, including MuJoCo, the DeepMind Control Suite, and a real-world dynamic object translation task with a robotic manipulator. Experimental results demonstrate that this simple mechanism improves learning performance over standard and self-imitation-based baselines.

</details>


### [87] [Pathology Transport: Optimal-Transport Explanations for Clinical Data, and When Their Heatmaps (Fail to) Localize Disease](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17370)
*Lalit Kumar*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 生成模型虽有望用于可解释临床AI，但本文通过实验揭示其解释热图存在合成到真实的显著差距：无标签热图在合成病变上表现良好，在真实病灶上却失效，仅监督方法保持有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式可解释AI方法大多仅展示热图外观，却很少严格验证其是否真正定位疾病；本文旨在系统检验最优传输生成模型产生的解释是否具有真实定位能力。

Method: 构建基于最优传输的整流流模型，在表格肿瘤标志物和胸部X光两类数据上训练，分别生成反事实样本、无监督恶性评分和重建保身份热图，并用指向游戏与放射科医生标注框进行定量评估。

Result: 表格数据上无监督评分AUROC达0.91-0.93，与监督分类器相关性约0.5；X光上传输热图为群体级信号而非局部定位器，重建变体在合成病变指向游戏0.52，但在真实RSNA标注上退化为随机水平，仅Grad-CAM有效。

Conclusion: 生成式解释热图在合成病灶上的显著表现不能证明其真实定位能力，必须通过受控基准进行压力测试；本文提供了可复用的生成解释流程和评估框架，强调无标签定位方法在临床落地前需谨慎验证。

Abstract: Generative models promise a route to explainable clinical AI: rather than probe a classifier, model the distributions of healthy and diseased patients and read explanations off the geometry between them. We build such a system - an optimal-transport rectified flow trained between two clinical distributions - and use it to ask a pointed question the field too rarely tests: do the resulting explanation heatmaps actually localize disease? On tabular tumour biomarkers (Breast Cancer Wisconsin) a single flow yields per-patient counterfactuals, an unsupervised malignancy score (AUROC 0.91; 0.93 +/- 0.01 across five seeds), and a label-free attribution that agrees with a supervised classifier (r ~ 0.5) - a compact, honest interpretability engine, though it never out-predicts logistic regression. Moving to chest X-rays, we show the transport heatmap is a population-level signal, not a localiser; a reconstruction-based, identity-preserving variant does localize synthetic lesions (pointing game 0.52), yet on real RSNA radiologist boxes it collapses to chance while only supervised Grad-CAM stays above it. The central result is a synthetic-to-real gap: label-free heatmaps that look compelling on planted lesions are not evidence of real localisation. We contribute a reusable optimal-transport recipe for generative explanations and a controlled benchmark for stress-testing whether they localize.

</details>


### [88] [MoNe: Modular Neural Memory for Efficient Long Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17616)
*Wonguk Cho,Kyubyung Chae,Tribhuvanesh Orekondy,Sunghyun Park,Hyoungwoo Park,Jeongho Kim,Arash Behboodi,Kyuwoong Hwang,Sungrack Yun*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出MoNe，一种轻量级模块化神经记忆，通过测试时学习和层局部梯度更新，使冻结的预训练Transformer无需重训练即可支持长上下文推理，实现O(N)预处理和O(1)查询成本，且峰值GPU内存不随上下文长度增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer在长上下文推理时，因自注意力机制导致计算和内存成本随序列长度线性或二次增长，而直接扩展窗口或重训练代价高昂；需一种轻量、即插即用的方法在不改动骨干模型的前提下高效处理超长上下文。

Method: MoNe采用两阶段设计：预处理阶段将上下文分段读取，通过测试时学习训练快速权重神经记忆网络，并采用层局部化梯度更新；推理阶段仅由查询token生成键和值，不再重读任何上下文token，从而将推理成本与上下文长度解耦。

Result: 在128K token下，相比上下文学习（ICL），MoNe将计算和峰值GPU内存均降低约80%，仅引入6.4%参数开销；在RULER的针在海草堆和单词提取基准上，MoNe表现出强泛化能力，超越ICL在超过骨干网络原生窗口长度时的严重退化。

Conclusion: MoNe证明通过轻量模块化记忆机制，可在不重新训练骨干模型的情况下高效扩展上下文长度，显著降低长上下文推理的资源消耗，同时保持强性能，为长上下文Transformer应用提供实用方案。

Abstract: We present MoNe, a lightweight modular neural memory that attaches to any frozen pretrained Transformer to enable long-context inference without retraining. MoNe reads context in fixed-size segments via test-time learning of fast-weight neural memory networks with layer-localized gradient updates; at inference, the memory generates keys and values from the query tokens alone, with no context tokens re-read. This two-phase design decouples inference cost from context length, achieving $O(N)$ preprocessing and $O(1)$ query cost with peak GPU memory that does not grow with $N$. At 128K tokens, MoNe reduces both compute and peak GPU memory by approximately 80% compared to ICL with only 6.4% parameter overhead. MoNe generalizes to context lengths far beyond the backbone's native window, achieving strong performance on needle-in-a-haystack and word extraction benchmarks from RULER, where ICL degrades sharply.

</details>


### [89] [GUPO: Gradient Uncertainty-aware Policy Optimization for Post-Training Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17411)
*Peizheng Guo,Jianqi Zhang,Xingyu Zhang,Yun Fan,Jiahuan Zhou,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: GRPO中不同查询的组梯度可能相互冲突，影响策略更新效率。为此提出GUPO，基于贝叶斯和Dirichlet分布建模组梯度不确定性并校准聚合权重，在多个基准上取得更好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO直接平均组梯度，但冲突的梯度方向会降低更新有效性。现有方法未考虑组梯度的可靠性差异，需要一种能处理冲突并可靠聚合更新的方法。

Method: GUPO将每个组梯度视为随机变量，采用贝叶斯建模估计其概率分布，然后利用Dirichlet分布推导梯度不确定性，据此调整各梯度在聚合中的贡献权重。

Result: 在多个基准上的大量实验表明，GUPO相比标准GRPO等基线方法，在推理任务上取得了更优的性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模和利用梯度不确定性，可以有效缓解组梯度冲突问题，提升策略优化的稳定性和效果。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has become a widely used approach for post-training Large Language Models (LLMs) for reasoning. In GRPO, the group gradients induced by different queries within the same mini-batch are directly averaged to form the policy update. However, these group gradients can point in conflicting directions. Our empirical analysis suggests that group-gradient conflicts tend to be associated with less effective policy updates, motivating the need for a reliable aggregated update direction under such conflicts. Standard GRPO aggregation treats the realized group gradients as deterministic contributions and does not account for differences in their reliability during aggregation. To address this issue, we propose Gradient Uncertainty-Aware Policy Optimization (GUPO), which models each group gradient as a random variable under a Bayesian formulation and estimates its probability distribution. GUPO then derives gradient uncertainty using a Dirichlet-based formulation and uses it to calibrate the contribution of each group gradient during aggregation. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of GUPO.

</details>


### [90] [Beyond the Trace: Coupling an Interpretable Reasoning-State Readout to Native MoE Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17638)
*Kang Chen,Sihan Zhao,Yixin Cao,Yugang Jiang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合专家推理模型的两级内部读取方法，通过将高维语义空间压缩为64轴框架（J64），并利用专家路由统计重建低开销代理（R64），从而揭示模型推理过程中的隐含状态，提升测试时决策和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理模型仅依赖输出文本，无法捕捉推理过程中的动态状态。作者希望利用混合专家模型的内部结构，提取可读、可操作的过程信号，以改进推理效率和准确性。

Method: 首先将词汇级J空间蒸馏为64轴语义框架J64，从模型自身的推理状态中学习。然后基于专家路由统计重建J64，得到低开销代理R64。在测试时，利用J64和R64进行单分支选择、加权投票、滚动窗口停止与重采样策略，并基于J64机制进行路由器编辑以验证因果性。

Result: J64在held-out AUC上比基线提升0.096至0.135；R64与J64的每轴中位数相关性为0.69至0.86，且在gpt-oss-20b上保留95%至100%的预测增益。R64加权投票在八种设置中的七种优于多数投票。J64在生成准确率上提升1.1至5.9点，R64保留0.9至3.2点。路由器编辑可诱发预期的推理行为变化。

Conclusion: J64使潜在过程状态变得可读，而路由机制使其可部署和可操作，从而显著提升推理模型的测试时决策能力和生成质量。

Abstract: What a reasoning model writes is only a partial record of the process that produces it. We introduce a two-level internal readout for mixture-of-experts reasoning. We first distill vocabulary-scale J-space into J64, a 64-axis semantic frame learned from the model's own reasoning states. J64 reveals readable process state that the emitted trace does not show: it separates inference effort from problem-induced strain. It also adds 0.096 to 0.135 held-out AUC over a baseline that reads the same rollout as token occupancy and aggregates it in exactly the same way. We then reconstruct J64 from native expert-routing statistics. The result is R64, a low-overhead proxy: its median per-axis correlation with J64 is 0.69 to 0.86 across three models and two families, and on gpt-oss-20b it preserves 95 to 100% of J64's predictive gain. The readout supports test-time decisions at two temporal resolutions. Over completed candidate sets, J64 and R64 improve single-branch selection, and R64-weighted voting improves plain majority voting in seven of eight settings. During generation, rolling readout windows drive a cumulative stop-and-resample policy whose operating point is fixed on training questions alone. J64 improves accuracy by 1.1 to 5.9 points over a sibling-permuted control, and the routing-only R64 proxy retains 0.9 to 3.2 of those points. Finally, router edits aimed at the mechanism J64 names induce the predicted reasoning behaviors and shift a diagnosed stall from numerical guessing toward exact symbolic execution. Together, J64 makes latent process state readable, while routing makes it deployable and actionable.

</details>


### [91] [Causal Local States: Scalable Simultaneous Causal Network Inference and Forecasting for Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17452)
*Jonas Braun,Fabian Fischbach,Daniel Köglmayr,Sebastian Baur,Christoph Räth*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出因果局部状态（CLS）框架，同时推断近似格兰杰因果网络并预测系统动态，在三个基准上实现高保真网络重建和与真实网络模型相当的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习预测缺乏可解释性，因果发现忽略预测能力，而结合两者的方法依赖单一全局超参数，无法处理异质系统结构。

Method: 对每个节点独立选择最小邻域集合，使预测模型接近最优地预测该节点，然后将各节点邻域组合用于全系统预测。

Result: 在三个难度递增的基准上，网络重建保真度高，预测精度与已知真实网络的模型持平。

Conclusion: CLS框架为可解释且可扩展的复杂系统预测提供了有效步骤，兼顾结构推断与预测性能。

Abstract: Machine learning methods predict many real-world systems with remarkable accuracy, but they are typically treated as black boxes that offer no insight into which interactions drive the dynamics. Causal discovery methods reconstruct the interaction network from observational data, but without regard to whether the inferred structure supports prediction. Existing approaches combining both tasks rely on a single global hyperparameter, such as a causal threshold or a fixed neighborhood size, which cannot recover the structure of heterogeneous systems. Here we introduce causal local states (CLS), a framework that simultaneously infers an approximate Granger-causal interaction network and forecasts the system dynamics. For each node independently, we select the smallest set of neighbors that allows a predictive model to forecast the node near-optimally, and the resulting neighborhoods are then combined for a forecast of the full system. On three benchmarks of increasing difficulty, we achieve reconstruction of the underlying networks with high fidelity and forecasts on par with a model that is supplied with the true network, providing a step toward explainable and scalable forecasting of complex systems.

</details>


### [92] [No Gaussian Required: Contrastive Inverse Dynamics for JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17542)
*Jack Boylan,Chris Hokamp*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出AC-MTM方法，用基于动作对比的逆向动力学训练目标替代SIGReg正则化，在保持JEPA架构和计算开销不变的情况下，防止潜在表征坍缩，并在多物体视觉任务上显著优于SIGReg。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA类世界模型依赖防坍缩机制，现有方法（如SIGReg）通过人为指定潜在分布形状来稳定表征，与所建模的环境无关，可能成为性能瓶颈。作者提出从转移数据本身提取防坍缩信号，更自然且无需额外超参数。

Method: AC-MTM保留LeWM的前向潜在预测目标，额外增加一个仅训练阶段使用的逆向动力学头，通过Action-NCE对比损失训练，要求每个潜在转移能区分产生它的动作。该逆向分支训练后丢弃，测试时编码、预测、规划与LeWM完全一致，且无需目标网络、stop-gradient、预训练编码器或重建目标。

Result: 在四个标准像素控制任务上，AC-MTM平均性能与SIGReg持平；在更难的多物体OGBench Visual Scene任务上，AC-MTM成功率80.0±2.0%，SIGReg为58.0±2.0%，每个种子改进20-24个百分点，随机策略基线为52%。

Conclusion: 基于对比逆向动力学的防坍缩信号是分布自由的，不依赖人为指定的表征几何，在复杂任务中更有效。作者还刻画了该方法成立所需的动作空间和可观测性假设。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) learn world models by predicting future embeddings, but the objective admits a trivial solution of a constant encoder, so every practical system adds an anti-collapse mechanism (LeCun, 2022; Assran et al., 2023; Bardes et al., 2022; 2024). LeWorldModel (LeWM) prevents collapse with SIGReg, a regularizer that forces the latent distribution to match an isotropic Gaussian: the representation is stabilized by prescribing what it must look like, independently of the environment it models. We argue that the anti-collapse pressure can instead come from the transition data itself. Action-Contrastive Masked Transition Modeling (AC-MTM) keeps LeWM's forward latent-prediction objective and adds a training-only inverse-dynamics head trained with Action-NCE: each latent transition must identify the action that produced it among the other actions in the batch, a discrimination task that a collapsed encoder provably fails. The inverse branch is discarded after training, leaving test-time encoding, forward prediction, planning, and compute identical to LeWM. On four standard pixel-control tasks under a matched planning protocol, AC-MTM trains stably from scratch and matches SIGReg on average. On the harder multi-object OGBench Visual Scene task, results are consistent with the prescribed geometry becoming a bottleneck: AC-MTM reaches 80.0$\pm$2.0% success versus 58.0$\pm$2.0% for SIGReg, improving by 20-24 points in each training seed. A single 50-episode random-policy run gives a 52% baseline estimate. Contrastive inverse dynamics thus provides a distribution-free anti-collapse signal that requires no target network, stop-gradient, pretrained encoder, or reconstruction objective, and we characterize the action-space and observability assumptions under which it holds. We make our code available at https://github.com/jackboyla/action-contrastive-jepa

</details>


### [93] [Elimination Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17646)
*Mian Huang,Xueqin Wang*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文提出消除几何（EG）框架，用于分析局部最优对象能否被单一部署规则实现，区分架构障碍与模型误差，并通过显式证书指导架构修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法常将性能缺陷笼统归因于模型或数据，缺乏系统性框架识别优化、压缩或消除过程中丢失的任务关键区分，以及这些损失是否可通过改变信息、架构或部署域来修复。

Method: EG建立类型化、原生损失、可审计的几何框架，分离局部可解性、全局可实现性和有限样本可证性，定义原生缺陷，并综合几何、优化、信息论、统计和机器学习工具，形成可积性、表示可允许性、资源约束等接口。

Result: 形式化结果覆盖正则、协调、奇异、组合和资源受限机制，应用涉及稀疏模型选择、无分布预测、观察性治疗策略等；可复现实验展示了证书如何引导架构修复并记录失败门。

Conclusion: 在归因持续性性能下限给架构前，必须固定部署契约、原生端点、竞争解释、信息与计算预算及验证规则，否则架构障碍与模型误差无法可靠区分。

Abstract: This monograph develops elimination geometry (EG), a typed, native-loss, audit-oriented framework for studying when locally optimal objects can be realized by a shared deployment rule. Elimination and compression may erase distinctions required by prediction, inference, control, or representation. EG asks which distinctions are lost, whether the induced defect is visible to the declared task, and whether changing information, architecture, action space, or deployment domain can repair it. EG separates local solvability, global realizability, and finite-sample certifiability. It derives native defects from the original objective and distinguishes architecture obstruction from model approximation, generalization, and implementation error. The monograph synthesizes tools from geometry, optimization, information theory, statistics, and machine learning into interfaces for integrability, representation admissibility, resource constraints, observational overlap, and common deployment. Formal results address regular, coordination, singular, compositional, and resource-limited mechanisms with explicit antecedents and claim boundaries. Applications include sparse model selection, distribution-free prediction, observational treatment policies, routed expert and retrieval systems, and learned score fields. Obstruction-Aware Learning and Inference links structural diagnosis to finite-data authorization, mechanism-matched intervention, and independent validation. Reproducible synthetic and real-data studies illustrate how certificates can guide architecture repair while recording failed gates and unresolved cases. The framework requires the deployment contract, native endpoint, competing explanations, information and compute budgets, and validation rule to be fixed before a persistent performance floor is attributed to architecture.

</details>


### [94] [Picard Proximal Monte Carlo for Parallel Bayesian Imaging with Score-Based Generative Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17666)
*Deliang Wei,Evan Bell,Wenhan Guo,Yifan Chen,Yu Sun*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于近端Langevin动力学与Picard迭代的时间并行后验采样框架，在保证重建质量的同时显著提升成像逆问题的求解速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯成像逆问题需要从高维后验分布中采样，现有基于分数或扩散模型的采样过程本质上是串行的，计算成本高，难以用于大规模成像应用。

Method: 采用近端似然公式以利用成像问题中的高效近端算子，通过Picard迭代在离散时间节点间暴露并行性，并支持多GPU实现；同时开发多块和退火变体以增强可扩展性与采样性能。

Result: 在多样成像逆问题实验中，PiX-MC大幅减少墙钟时间且保持重建质量；在512×512×80稀疏视图CT问题上，退火多块PiX-MC在八GPU下较标准Langevin采样器实现最高50倍加速。

Conclusion: 该框架是高效且可扩展的后验采样方法，兼顾并行性与理论收敛保证，适用于大规模成像逆问题。

Abstract: Bayesian imaging inverse problems often require sampling from high-dimensional posterior distributions. While recent score-based and diffusion models provide expressive Bayesian priors, their sampling procedures remain inherently sequential and computationally expensive for large-scale imaging applications. We propose PiX-MC, a time-parallel posterior sampling framework based on proximal Langevin dynamics and Picard iteration. The proximal-likelihood formulation exploits the fact that many imaging likelihoods admit efficient, problem-specific proximal operators, while Picard refinement exposes parallelism across discretization nodes and naturally supports multi-GPU implementation. To further improve practical scalability and sampling performance, we develop multi-block and annealed variants of the proposed framework. We establish convergence guarantees under transparent assumptions, accommodating non-log-concave posteriors, imperfect learned score models, multi-block implementations, and annealing schedules. Experiments on a diverse collection of imaging inverse problems demonstrate that PiX-MC substantially reduces wall-clock time while preserving reconstruction quality. On a $512\times512\times80$ sparse-view computed tomography (CT) problem, annealed multi-block PiX-MC achieves up to a $50\times$ runtime speedup over the standard Langevin sampler using eight GPUs.

</details>


### [95] [Neuro-symbolic learning over OWL 2 DL via consequence-based compilation to differentiable circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17741)
*Olga Mashkova,Asaad Mohammedsaleh,Fernando Zhapa-Camacho,Robert Hoehndorf*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出Baobab系统，将SROIQ本体编译为命题决策图（SDD），通过加权模型计数训练感知网络，并首次在非Horn描述逻辑中刻画和缓解推理捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号方法要么嵌入连续空间放弃经典推理，要么局限于Horn片段EL++，无法处理SROIQ的完整表达能力；且存在推理捷径问题导致感知网络退化。

Method: 将SROIQ本体与有限ABox编译为SDD：先用基于后果的演算饱和命题核心，再对活动域实例化剩余特征（nominals、数量限制、角色公理）。利用证据条件下的加权模型计数训练CNN，并用按查询理由（justifications）索引的混合模型校准后验。

Result: 在覆盖所有SROIQ特征的MNIST任务上，CNN成功学习识别数字及后继关系并恢复潜在概念；混合模型达到贝叶斯最优后验，而单次WMC和BEARS集成均失败。编译器与表示定理经Lean 4机器验证。

Conclusion: Baobab首次实现非Horn描述逻辑下的可靠神经符号推理，并系统化解决推理捷径，为可验证的混合AI系统提供新范式。

Abstract: OWL 2 DL ontologies, grounded in the description logic $\mathcal{SROIQ}$, express large knowledge bases in biomedicine and the Semantic Web. Neuro-symbolic (NeSy) learners over description logics either embed the ontology in a continuous space, abandoning classical entailment, or restrict to the Horn fragment $\mathcal{EL}^{++}$, which has a single canonical model. We present Baobab, which compiles a $\mathcal{SROIQ}$ ontology with a finite ABox into a Sentential Decision Diagram (SDD): it saturates a propositional core under a consequence-based calculus and instantiates the remaining $\mathcal{SROIQ}$ features (nominals, number restrictions, and the role axioms) over the active domain. The SDD's evidence-conditioned weighted model count then trains a perception network to recognize real images under partial ABox supervision: on an ontology that exercises every distinctive $\mathcal{SROIQ}$ feature, a CNN learns to read MNIST digits coupled by a successor relation and recovers latent ontology concepts that an independent perception leaves at chance. When the supervision admits several ontology-consistent completions, an independent perception collapses onto one, a reasoning shortcut: we show that a mixture indexed by the query's justifications can represent the calibrated posterior no independent perception can, and that seeding it from the circuit's enumerated completions attains the Bayes-optimal posterior on a real-image MNIST task where single-WMC and learned mixtures (the BEARS-ensemble hypothesis class) do not: to our knowledge the first to characterize and mitigate reasoning shortcuts in a non-Horn description logic. Soundness of the compiler and the representation result are machine-checked in Lean 4. Code is available at https://github.com/bio-ontology-research-group/baobab.

</details>


### [96] [D$^2$ACCI: A Dual-Loop Diagnostic Protocol for Evidence-Preserving Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17756)
*Xule Liu,Yijun Liu,Chao Li,Shao Kun*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出D²ACCI双环协议，用于LLM代理持久记忆系统的诊断驱动迭代，结合DCR可观测性指标和可复用评估工件，在三个基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久记忆系统多阶段管道（摄取、检索、过滤、生成）中故障难以定位，端到端评估只能指出错误发生，无法确定阶段；现有评估缺乏统计比较、切片级非回归检查和阶段级诊断痕迹。

Method: D²ACCI包含外层诊断门（基于配对证据、保护切片监控、痕迹级可定位性来促进、标记或拒绝记忆干预）和DCR分阶可观测性指标，以及可重放的D²ACCI-Eval工件；实例化为MemStack并应用。

Result: 在LoCoMo、LongMemEval、PersonaMem-V2上分别达到93.59%、90.93%、57.20%；五个配对消融显示补充提取、会话记忆检索和遗忘门带来统计显著提升（+1.9至+3.7pp，p≤.003）；BM25/RRF作为受监控的特征标志被保留；诊断审计中丰富痕迹显著提高根因一致性，诊断工件DCR@3达98-100%，而仅结果日志为0%。

Conclusion: 稳健的记忆系统迭代需要可追踪、统计基础扎实且回归感知的证据，D²ACCI正好填补了这一空白。

Abstract: Memory is a key capability of LLM agents. Persistent memory extends this across sessions---enabling recall, revision, and personalization. Yet its multi-stage pipeline (ingestion, retrieval, filtering, generation) makes failures difficult to localize: end-to-end evaluation reveals that an error occurred, but not which stage caused it. Existing evaluations often report aggregate performance without paired statistical comparisons, slice-level non-regression checks, or stage-level diagnostic traces. We propose D$^2$ACCI (Diagnostic-Driven Artifact-based Closed-loop Controlled Iteration), a dual-loop protocol whose outer diagnostic gate promotes, feature-flags, or rejects memory interventions based on paired evidence, protected-slice monitoring, and trace-level localizability. We further introduce DCR, a graded observability metric that measures whether failures remain localizable, and D$^2$ACCI-Eval, a reusable artifact for gate replay. We instantiate the protocol in MemStack and evaluate on three public benchmarks, achieving 93.59% on LoCoMo, 90.93% on LongMemEval, and 57.20% on PersonaMem-V2. Five paired ablations show that supplement extraction, session-memory retrieval, and Forget Guard yield statistically significant gains (+1.9 to +3.7pp, all p $\le$ .003). In contrast, BM25/RRF is retained as a monitored feature flag---a distinction invisible to aggregate-only evaluation. A diagnostic audit shows enriched traces substantially improve root-cause agreement over result-only relabeling. Diagnostic artifacts reach 98--100% DCR@3 versus 0% for results-only logs. These results establish that robust memory-system iteration demands traceable, statistically grounded, and regression-aware evidence---exactly the gap D$^2$ACCI fills.

</details>


### [97] [Training-Free Human-in-the-Loop Anomaly Detection via Memory Bank Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17775)
*Ayusha Abbas,Saram Abbas,Kabita Adhikari*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的基于人工反馈的异常检测框架，通过直接编辑PatchCore的内存库来修正检测器，在仅用十个黄金样本的情况下，人工修正使中位性能差距缩小66%，并显著优于未修正的数百样本训练结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常检测器在训练数据最稀缺的场景下最难部署，如新投产的生产线只有少量已验证的黄金样本且现场无机器学习工程师，传统训练方法不可行。

Method: 训练无关的闭环人工框架，领域专家通过直接编辑内存库修正PatchCore检测器，无需重训练或梯度。误报修正通过自校准新颖性门控，仅将超过中位近邻距离的图像补丁加入库中。

Result: 在15个MVTec AD类别中显著改善12类且无损害；十样本加修正优于数百样本无修正。对于已训练库，改进空间较小且集中在采样不足的类别，仅grid类别轻微受损。使用保留协议评估以避免记忆化导致的AUROC虚高，被动与主动查询无显著差异，缺陷记忆扩展失败。

Conclusion: 该框架在小样本场景下高效且无需训练，但活体专家试验中标签错误对小库的负面影响仍是未来研究方向。

Abstract: Anomaly detectors are hardest to deploy exactly where training data is scarcest: a newly commissioned production line has a handful of verified "golden" samples and no machine-learning engineer on the factory floor. We present a training-free human-in-the-loop framework in which a domain expert corrects a PatchCore detector by direct memory bank editing: no retraining, no gradients, no original training data. A false-positive correction inserts the reviewed image's normal patches through a self-calibrating novelty gate admitting only those beyond the median pool-normal nearest-neighbour distance. From a bank built on only ten golden samples, operator corrections close a median 66% of the gap to an uncorrected fully trained bank (mean 80%, raised by three categories that overshoot parity), significantly improving 12 of 15 MVTec AD categories and harming none: ten samples plus corrections outperform hundreds of samples without them. On already-trained banks the headroom is smaller and concentrated where the bank undersamples normal appearance (gated: toothbrush +0.10, metal nut +0.09, zipper +0.05, screw +0.05), and no category except grid is significantly harmed. Evaluation uses a held-out protocol (20 splits per category, Holm-corrected Wilcoxon), because corrected images entering the bank inflate naive evaluation toward AUROC 1.0 by memorisation. Passive and active querying are statistically indistinguishable; a matched-label-budget control attributes gains to deployment-time label production at 43% of exhaustive-review cost; a defect-memory extension fails decisively. Feedback is simulated from ground truth; live expert trials, where mislabelling is costliest on small banks, remain future work.

</details>


### [98] [ARASH: Adaptive Retrieval And Shot Selection for Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17856)
*Samirasadat Jamalidinan,Yue Xu,Kazem Cheshmi*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出ARASH方法，通过局部邻域分析为TabPFN选择最优示例，将提示长度和内存占用分别减少1261.5倍和2.56倍，同时保持相近的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格预测任务中，大型语言模型和专用表格基础模型（如TabPFN）虽有效但计算资源消耗大且重训练不现实。上下文学习（尤其是少样本提示）提供资源高效替代方案，但如何选择最相关的数据行作为示例仍具挑战。

Method: ARASH（自适应、查询特定的检索与示例选择）方法，基于训练集内的局部邻域分析，为每个查询自适应地选择最优示例行，以提升表格基础模型的提示效率。

Result: 在实验中，ARASH使TabPFN的提示长度缩减1261.5倍，内存使用减少2.56倍，同时准确率与原始方法相当。

Conclusion: ARASH通过智能示例选择显著提升表格基础模型的资源效率，在不牺牲精度的情况下实现更实用的部署，尤其适用于计算资源受限的场景。

Abstract: Tabular prediction is a critical task across numerous applications. The recent success of large language models has sparked various approaches for adapting them to the tabular domain. A prevalent strategy involves training or fine-tuning specialized Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) such as TabPFN. However, TFMs require substantial computational resources, and frequent model retraining is often impractical. In-context learning (ICL), specifically, few-shot prompting, offers a resource-efficient alternative to enhance performance. Yet, identifying the most relevant rows to serve as shots remains a challenge for tabular data. This paper introduces ARASH (Adaptive, query-specific Retrieval And Shot selection), a method that improves TFM efficiency by selecting optimal shots based on local neighborhood analysis within the training set. Our results demonstrate that ARASH reduces the prompt length and memory usage of TabPFN by 1261.5$\times$ and 2.56$\times$, respectively, while providing comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [99] [Adaptive Policy Portfolios for Robust Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17929)
*Kasper Engelen,Sebastian Junges,Guillermo A. Pérez,Marnix Suilen*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 本文研究鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（RMDP）中的自适应策略组合，提出用鲁棒遗憾度量组合质量，并给出组合验证与合成的计算复杂性结果，同时提出一种可运行时特化的离线构建方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RMDP优化单一策略对一组转移函数鲁棒，但当真实动态固定且部署后可部分识别时，这种策略可能过于保守。自适应策略组合（离线合成多个策略，在线轻量选择）能平衡鲁棒性与保守性，但缺乏对其理论复杂性和系统构建方法的研究。

Method: 定义鲁棒遗憾作为组合质量的度量；从计算复杂性角度分析组合验证（给定组合是否满足鲁棒遗憾？）和组合合成（寻找最优组合）的难度；针对验证问题归结为$\forall\mathbb{R}$-完全，针对合成问题归结为$\exists\forall\mathbb{R}$-完全；并提出一种离线组合构建算法，支持运行时特化。

Result: 验证给定组合在确定性组合和无环(s,a)-矩形RMDP中已是$\forall\mathbb{R}$-完全；合成单例有界大小的组合在一般有理多面体、固定折扣和无环动态下为$\exists\forall\mathbb{R}$-完全；单策略情况同时具备组合和代数困难；提出的离线构建方法可行且支持运行时特化。

Conclusion: 自适应策略组合虽能缓解RMDP的保守性，但其验证与合成问题在计算上高度困难（涉及实数复杂性类），需要近似或启发式方法；所提离线构建方法为实际应用提供了一条可行路径，但仍需进一步研究近似算法和可扩展实现。

Abstract: Robust Markov decision processes optimize one policy against a set of plausible transition functions. This can be conservative when the unknown dynamics are fixed and become partially identifiable after deployment. We study adaptive policy portfolios: finite sets of memoryless randomized policies synthesized offline and paired with a lightweight online selector. Robust regret is a natural measure of portfolio quality: for each plausible environment, it measures the loss of the best portfolio member relative to the policy that would have been optimal had that environment been known. Related regret objectives were studied by Ghavamzadeh et al. (2016) with an emphasis on approximations and relaxations for safe policy improvement. We give a complexity-theoretic account of portfolio certification and synthesis. Certifying a given portfolio is $\forall\mathbb{R}$-complete already for deterministic portfolios in acyclic (s,a)-rectangular RMDPs. Synthesizing a portfolio of unary-bounded size is $\exists\forall\mathbb{R}$-complete for general rational polytopes, even with fixed discount and acyclic dynamics. The single-policy case is already hard, both combinatorially and algebraically. Finally, we present an offline portfolio construction that is amenable to runtime specialization.

</details>


### [100] [EvoTS-Agent: A Self-Evolving LLM Agent for Financial Time Series Change Point Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17933)
*Lei Jiang,Ye Wei,Xinyu Xi,Jordan Langham-Lopez,Yifan Bao,Raad Khraishi,Yihao Ang,Anthony K. H. Tung,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出EvoTS-Agent，一个基于验证反馈自我进化的LLM代理，用于金融时间序列变点检测，通过三种演化算子自动构建检测流程，在四个基准数据集上超越现有LLM代理且保持100%执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列具有非平稳和异质性统计特征，单一无监督算法无法在不同资产和市场状态下一致表现，传统工作流依赖专家进行模型选择、特征设计和超参调优，缺乏可扩展性和适应性。

Method: EvoTS-Agent先进行探索性数据分析以刻画数据集属性并初始化候选模型，然后通过三种互补算子演化实验轨迹：Revision利用当前最优解，Alternative Strategy在进展停滞时探索根本不同的建模方向，Recombination综合高表现轨迹的互补证据；验证反馈全程引导轨迹演化，使流程适配各数据集统计特征。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，EvoTS-Agent一致优于现有LLM代理，并在所有评估的后端LLM上保持100%的执行成功率。

Conclusion: 验证引导的自我演化机制能有效增强LLM代理在金融时间序列变点检测中的自主性和鲁棒性，减少人工干预，提升跨数据集适应性。

Abstract: Financial time series exhibit non-stationary and heterogeneous statistical properties, making change-point detection challenging because no single unsupervised algorithm performs consistently across assets and market regimes. Conventional workflows consequently depend heavily on expert-driven model selection, feature design, and hyperparameter tuning, limiting their scalability and adaptability. We propose EvoTS-Agent, a validation-guided self-evolving LLM agent for autonomous financial time-series change-point detection. EvoTS-Agent first performs curated exploratory data analysis to characterize dataset properties and initialize candidate detection models. It then evolves executable experiment trajectories through three complementary operators: \textit{Revision} exploits the current best solution, \textit{Alternative Strategy} explores fundamentally different modeling directions when progress stagnates, and \textit{Recombination} synthesizes complementary evidence from high-performing trajectories. Validation feedback guides trajectory evolution throughout the search, enabling the agent to adapt its detection pipeline to the statistical characteristics of each dataset while preserving reliable optimization. Experiments across four benchmark datasets demonstrate that EvoTS-Agent consistently outperforms existing LLM-based agents while maintaining a 100\% execution success rate across all evaluated backbone LLMs.

</details>


### [101] [Towards Zero-Shot Task Transfer with Neurosymbolic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17959)
*Isidoro Tamassia,Lennert De Smet,Giuseppe Marra*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号世界模型，将奖励预测与观测重建解耦，使模型能零样本适应新奖励函数，优于纯神经方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型强化学习的世界模型依赖任务且潜在表征不可解释，难以泛化到新任务。

Method: 将潜在状态分为结构化符号子集和其余部分，奖励预测仅依赖符号组件，观测重建独立进行，形成神经符号世界模型。

Result: 实验证明该方法在零样本适应新奖励函数时具有强泛化性，优于纯神经方法。

Conclusion: 神经符号世界模型通过解耦奖励预测与观测重建，实现高效的任务泛化，虽有挑战但潜力显著。

Abstract: State-of-the-art model-based reinforcement learning methods learn neural world models that allow policy improvement by planning in a latent space, without assumptions on the structure of the underlying environment. While expressive, these models are generally task-dependent: they learn uninterpretable latent representations that are tied to the training task and thus hard to generalize to new tasks. In this work, we present a novel world model formulation where the reward prediction only depends on a subset of structured, symbolic components of the whole latent state. Decoupling observation reconstruction and reward prediction allows us to learn world models that can adapt zero-shot, i.e. without further environment interactions, to new reward functions defined over the same symbolic state space. We discuss the main advantages and challenges of learning these neurosymbolic world models and demonstrate the strong generalisation properties of our approach over purely neural methods.

</details>


### [102] [MoRAX: Mobility-based Representation Augmentation for Geospatial Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17848)
*Ya Wen,Jixuan Cai,Yulun Zhou,Alec Kirkley*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: MoRAX是一个轻量级框架，通过人类移动性数据增强地理空间基础模型的嵌入表示，在保留城市覆盖和一致性的同时提供功能区连接信息，可在无移动数据的城市中零样本部署，并在多个预测任务中优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型依赖地球观测数据，缺乏人类活动信息，而人类移动数据能揭示区域间的功能和关系结构，但通常局限于观测城市，难以用于可迁移的城市表示学习。

Method: MoRAX引入教师-学生模型架构：教师模型利用移动性流调制GFM嵌入，捕捉城市功能区连接性；学生模型不接收移动性输入，通过蒸馏学习逼近教师性能，从而实现无移动数据城市的部署。

Result: 在两国四个目标城市的八项社会经济与环境预测任务中，教师模型持续优于GFM和强基线；学生模型在多数任务上接近教师性能。跨国家迁移结果验证了移动性条件调制的有效性。

Conclusion: 移动性流条件调制为地理空间基础模型补充人类维度信息提供了一种通用机制，增强了其在城市分析中的可迁移性和实用性。

Abstract: Geospatial Foundation Models (GFMs) are emerging as a powerful paradigm for learning semantically rich and geographically consistent visual and physical representations. However, their reliance on Earth-observation (EO) data leaves information about human activity largely underrepresented. Human mobility data reveals the functional and relational structure between regions that is missing from EO data, but is often limited only to the city where it is observed, making it challenging to use for transferable urban representation learning. We introduce MoRAX, a lightweight framework for augmenting geospatial embeddings with functional structure derived from human mobility. MoRAX preserves the coverage and consistency of a GFM while providing information about the functional connectivity among urban regions, permitting zero-shot deployment in unseen cities with or without available mobility data. Across four target cities spanning two countries, the MoRAX teacher model, which observes mobility, consistently outperforms GFMs and strong urban representation baselines in eight socioeconomic and environmental prediction tasks. Meanwhile, the student model, which never takes mobility data as input, approaches the teacher in performance on most tasks. Transfer results across countries further demonstrate that modulation conditioned on mobility flows provides a general mechanism for grounding geospatial foundations in the human dimension of cities.

</details>


### [103] [Dynamic Compression in Recurrent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17896)
*Jyothish Pari,Ryan Bahlous-Boldi,Pulkit Agrawal*

Main category: Inference

TL;DR: 提出动态压缩机制，允许循环模型通过额外循环更新选择性重访历史token并修正定长状态，在少样本任务中显著降低状态需求并提升可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统循环模型以单次因果扫描压缩历史，固定状态需为未知未来需求妥协，导致信息保真度受限。

Method: 模型保留原始序列，通过选择性重访相关token执行额外循环更新，动态调整定长状态。

Result: 在需存储多个函数并在后续少样本任务复用的设定中，动态压缩大幅降低所需状态大小，且随函数数量增多扩展性更优。

Conclusion: 揭示计算-记忆权衡：以更多计算换取固定大小状态的高效利用，为长上下文循环模型提供新方向。

Abstract: Recurrent models process long contexts efficiently by compressing their history into a fixed-size state, but modern architectures typically do so in a single causal pass over the sequence. Each input must therefore be compressed before the model knows how it will later be used, forcing a limited state to compromise across possible future demands. We introduce dynamic compression, which allows a recurrent model to selectively revisit past tokens and revise its fixed-size state through additional recurrent updates. The model need not preserve every part of the history at uniformly high fidelity in its recurrent state, because lower-fidelity information can be revisited from the retained raw sequence when it becomes relevant. We study this in a controlled setting where the model first learns multiple functions in-context and, later in the same sequence, encounters a series of few-shot tasks that each require it to identify and reuse one of those functions.
